// app/my-account/addresses/page.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";
import AuthGuard from "@/components/auth-guard";
import AccountSkeleton from "@/components/account-skeleton";
import SubmitButton from "@/components/submit-button";
import FieldError from "@/components/field-error";
import {
  getCustomer,
  updateCustomer,
  type Address,
  type Customer,
  type UpdateCustomerInput,
} from "@/lib/auth";
import { GraphQLError } from "@/lib/graphql";
import { normalizeKuwaitPhone } from "@/lib/checkout-validation";
import { KUWAIT_GOVERNORATES } from "@/lib/orders";

/* ============================================================
   TYPES & CONSTANTS
   ============================================================ */

/**
 * Single delivery address form.
 * Field names match checkout so both pages store the same data in WooCommerce.
 */
interface DeliveryAddressForm {
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  phone: string;
  address1: string;
  address2: string;
  governorate: string;
  area: string;
  deliveryNote: string;
}

type FormErrors = Partial<Record<keyof DeliveryAddressForm, string>>;

const KUWAIT_COUNTRY_CODE: string = "KW";
const KUWAIT_MOBILE_RE: RegExp = /^[569]\d{7}$/;

/* ============================================================
   HELPERS
   ============================================================ */

/**
 * Reads the saved shipping address into form values.
 * We store Kuwait address data in WooCommerce's "shipping" address:
 *   state   = governorate
 *   city    = area
 *   address1 = block, street, building
 *   address2 = floor, apartment, delivery note
 */
function toForm(address: Address | null | undefined): DeliveryAddressForm {
  return {
    firstName: address?.firstName ?? "",
    lastName: address?.lastName ?? "",
    phone: normalizeKuwaitPhone(address?.phone ?? ""),
    address1: address?.address1 ?? "",
    address2: address?.address2 ?? "",
    governorate: address?.state ?? "",
    area: address?.city ?? "",
    deliveryNote: "",
  };
}

/** Builds the WooCommerce address object. Empty strings clear old values. */
function buildAddress(form: DeliveryAddressForm): Address {
  const phone: string = normalizeKuwaitPhone(form.phone);
  return {
    firstName: form.firstName.trim(),
    lastName: form.lastName.trim(),
    company: "",
    address1: form.address1.trim(),
    address2: form.address2.trim(),
    city: form.area.trim(),
    state: form.governorate,
    postcode: "",
    country: KUWAIT_COUNTRY_CODE,
    phone: phone ? `+965${phone}` : "",
    email: "",
  };
}

/** Validates the form. Phone is optional but must be valid when entered. */
function validateForm(form: DeliveryAddressForm): FormErrors {
  const errors: FormErrors = {};

  if (!form.firstName.trim()) errors.firstName = "First name is required.";
  if (!form.lastName.trim()) errors.lastName = "Last name is required.";
  if (!form.governorate) errors.governorate = "Select your governorate.";
  if (!form.area.trim()) errors.area = "Area is required.";
  if (!form.address1.trim()) errors.address1 = "Block, street and building are required.";

  if (form.phone.trim() && !KUWAIT_MOBILE_RE.test(normalizeKuwaitPhone(form.phone))) {
    errors.phone = "Enter an 8-digit Kuwait mobile number (starts with 5, 6 or 9).";
  }

  return errors;
}

/**
 * Keeps a saved governorate in the dropdown even if it's not in the current list.
 */
function governorateOptions(current: string): string[] {
  const list: string[] = [...(KUWAIT_GOVERNORATES as ReadonlyArray<string>)];
  return current && !list.includes(current) ? [current, ...list] : list;
}

/**
 * Renders a saved address as static text.
 */
function SavedAddress({ address }: { address: Address }): ReactNode {
  return (
    <address className="not-italic text-sm text-[#212529] space-y-1">
      {(address.firstName || address.lastName) && (
        <p className="font-medium">
          {[address.firstName, address.lastName].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}
        </p>
      )}
      {address.phone && <p>{address.phone}</p>}
      {address.address1 && <p>{address.address1}</p>}
      {address.address2 && <p>{address.address2}</p>}
      <p>{[address.city, address.state].filter(Boolean).join(", ")}</p>
      <p>Kuwait</p>
    </address>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   FORM FIELDS
   ============================================================ */

function inputClass(hasError: boolean): string {
  return `w-full rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-sm text-[#212529] placeholder:text-[#6c757d] focus:border-[#e91e63] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#e91e63] ${
    hasError ? "border-red-500" : "border-[#dee2e6]"
  }`;
}

interface FieldProps {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  error?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  placeholder?: string;
  autoComplete?: string;
  inputMode?: "text" | "tel" | "numeric";
  type?: string;
  className?: string;
}

function Field({
  id,
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  error,
  required = false,
  placeholder,
  autoComplete,
  inputMode,
  type = "text",
  className = "",
}: FieldProps): ReactNode {
  const errorId: string = `${id}-error`;
  return (
    <div className={className}>
      <label htmlFor={id} className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-[#212529]">
        {label}
        {required && <span className="text-red-500"> *</span>}
      </label>
      <input
        id={id}
        name={id}
        type={type}
        inputMode={inputMode}
        value={value}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        autoComplete={autoComplete}
        required={required}
        aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
        aria-describedby={error ? errorId : undefined}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        className={inputClass(Boolean(error))}
      />
      <FieldError id={errorId} message={error} />
    </div>
  );
}

function SelectField({
  id,
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
  error,
  required = false,
}: {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  options: string[];
  error?: string;
  required?: boolean;
}): ReactNode {
  const errorId: string = `${id}-error`;
  return (
    <div>
      <label htmlFor={id} className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-[#212529]">
        {label}
        {required && <span className="text-red-500"> *</span>}
      </label>
      <select
        id={id}
        name={id}
        value={value}
        required={required}
        aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
        aria-describedby={error ? errorId : undefined}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        className={inputClass(Boolean(error))}
      >
        <option value="">Select</option>
        {options.map((option) => (
          <option key={option} value={option}>
            {option}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <FieldError id={errorId} message={error} />
    </div>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   PAGE
   ============================================================ */
export default function AddressesPage(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <AuthGuard>
      <AddressesContent />
    </AuthGuard>
  );
}

function AddressesContent(): ReactNode {
  const [customer, setCustomer] = useState<Customer | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let active: boolean = true;

    getCustomer()
      .then((data) => {
        if (active) setCustomer(data);
      })
      .catch((err: unknown) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setError(err instanceof GraphQLError ? err.message : "Could not load your address.");
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);

  if (loading) return <AccountSkeleton />;

  if (error || !customer) {
    return (
      <div
        role="alert"
        className="rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700"
      >
        {error || "Could not load customer data."}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-[#212529]">Delivery Address</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#6c757d]">
          Your saved delivery address for Kuwait orders. This address can be used
          during checkout.
        </p>
      </div>

      <DeliveryAddressSection
        address={customer.shipping ?? null}
        onSaved={setCustomer}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   DELIVERY ADDRESS SECTION
   Three states: empty (add), saved (view), and form (edit).
   ============================================================ */
function DeliveryAddressSection({
  address,
  onSaved,
}: {
  address: Address | null;
  onSaved: (customer: Customer) => void;
}): ReactNode {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<DeliveryAddressForm>(() => toForm(address));
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string>("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string>("");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const hasAddress: boolean = Boolean(address?.address1 || address?.city);

  /** Reset the form from saved data every time editing starts. */
  const startEditing = (): void => {
    setForm(toForm(address));
    setErrors({});
    setFormError("");
    setSuccess("");
    setEditing(true);
  };

  const cancelEditing = (): void => {
    setEditing(false);
    setErrors({});
    setFormError("");
  };

  const handleChange = (field: keyof DeliveryAddressForm, value: string): void => {
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));
    setErrors((prev) => {
      if (!prev[field]) return prev;
      const next: FormErrors = { ...prev };
      delete next[field];
      return next;
    });
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>): Promise<void> => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (saving) return;

    const nextErrors: FormErrors = validateForm(form);
    setErrors(nextErrors);
    setFormError("");

    const firstInvalid: string | undefined = Object.keys(nextErrors)[0];
    if (firstInvalid) {
      document.getElementById(`address-${firstInvalid}`)?.focus();
      return;
    }

    const input: UpdateCustomerInput = { shipping: buildAddress(form) };

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const updated: Customer = await updateCustomer(input);
      onSaved(updated);
      setEditing(false);
      setSuccess("Delivery address saved.");
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setFormError(
        err instanceof GraphQLError
          ? err.message
          : "Could not save your address. Please try again."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  /* ── Empty state ── */
  if (!editing && !hasAddress) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6] p-5">
        <p className="text-sm text-[#6c757d]">No delivery address saved yet.</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={startEditing}
          className="mt-4 text-sm font-medium text-[#e91e63] hover:underline focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#e91e63]"
        >
          Add address
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* ── Saved address (view) ── */
  if (!editing && address) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6] p-5">
        <SavedAddress address={address} />
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={startEditing}
          className="mt-4 text-sm font-medium text-[#e91e63] hover:underline focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#e91e63]"
        >
          Edit
        </button>
        {success && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-green-600">{success}</p>}
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* ── Edit form ── */
  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6] p-5">
      {formError && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="mb-4 rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700"
        >
          {formError}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <Field
          id="address-firstName"
          label="First name"
          required
          value={form.firstName}
          error={errors.firstName}
          autoComplete="given-name"
          onChange={(v) => handleChange("firstName", v)}
        />
        <Field
          id="address-lastName"
          label="Last name"
          required
          value={form.lastName}
          error={errors.lastName}
          autoComplete="family-name"
          onChange={(v) => handleChange("lastName", v)}
        />
        <Field
          id="address-phone"
          label="Mobile number"
          type="tel"
          inputMode="tel"
          value={form.phone}
          error={errors.phone}
          placeholder="5XXX XXXX"
          autoComplete="tel-national"
          onChange={(v) => handleChange("phone", v)}
        />
        <Field
          id="address-area"
          label="Area"
          required
          value={form.area}
          error={errors.area}
          placeholder="e.g. Salmiya"
          autoComplete="address-level2"
          onChange={(v) => handleChange("area", v)}
        />
        <SelectField
          id="address-governorate"
          label="Governorate"
          required
          value={form.governorate}
          error={errors.governorate}
          options={governorateOptions(form.governorate)}
          onChange={(v) => handleChange("governorate", v)}
        />
        <div /> {/* grid spacer */}
        <Field
          id="address-address1"
          label="Block, street and building"
          required
          value={form.address1}
          error={errors.address1}
          placeholder="e.g. Block 10, Street 5, Building 12"
          autoComplete="address-line1"
          className="sm:col-span-2"
          onChange={(v) => handleChange("address1", v)}
        />
        <Field
          id="address-address2"
          label="Floor and apartment (optional)"
          value={form.address2}
          placeholder="e.g. Floor 3, Apartment 7"
          autoComplete="address-line2"
          className="sm:col-span-2"
          onChange={(v) => handleChange("address2", v)}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex gap-3">
        <SubmitButton loading={saving} label="Save" />
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={cancelEditing}
          className="rounded-md border border-[#dee2e6] px-6 py-2.5 text-[14px] font-semibold uppercase text-[#6c757d] transition-colors hover:bg-gray-50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#e91e63]"
        >
          Cancel
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

File: app/my-account/details/page.tsx

// app/my-account/details/page.tsx
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import AuthGuard from "@/components/auth-guard";
import AccountSkeleton from "@/components/account-skeleton";
import SubmitButton from "@/components/submit-button";
import FieldError from "@/components/field-error";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/use-auth";
import { updateUser, sendPasswordResetEmail, type User } from "@/lib/auth";
import { GraphQLError } from "@/lib/graphql";
import { validateDetailsForm } from "@/lib/validators";

/* ── Shared styles (design system) ───────────────────────────── */

const INPUT_CLASS =
  "w-full rounded-md border border-[#dee2e6] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-[#212529] placeholder:text-[#6c757d] focus:border-[#e91e63] focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]";

const LABEL_CLASS = "mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-[#212529]";

const ALERT_ERROR_CLASS =
  "rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700";

const ALERT_SUCCESS_CLASS =
  "rounded-md border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-700";

/* ── Page ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

export default function DetailsPage() {
  return (
    <AuthGuard>
      <DetailsContent />
    </AuthGuard>
  );
}

function DetailsContent() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  if (loading) return <AccountSkeleton />;
  if (!user) return null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-10">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-[#212529]">Account Details</h2>
      <ProfileForm user={user} />
      <ChangePasswordSection username={user.username} />
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Profile form ────────────────────────────────────────────── */

function ProfileForm({ user }: { user: User }) {
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState(user.firstName ?? "");
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState(user.lastName ?? "");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(user.email ?? "");
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    const validation = validateDetailsForm({ firstName, lastName, email });
    setErrors(validation.errors);
    if (!validation.valid) return;

    setFormError("");
    setSuccess("");
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      await updateUser({
        id: user.id,
        firstName: firstName.trim() || undefined,
        lastName: lastName.trim() || undefined,
        email: email.trim(),
      });
      setSuccess("Account details saved.");
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setFormError(
        err instanceof GraphQLError ? err.message : "Failed to save details.",
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="max-w-lg space-y-4">
      {formError && (
        <div role="alert" className={ALERT_ERROR_CLASS}>
          {formError}
        </div>
      )}
      {success && (
        <div role="status" className={ALERT_SUCCESS_CLASS}>
          {success}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="details-first-name" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
            First name
          </label>
          <input
            id="details-first-name"
            name="firstName"
            type="text"
            autoComplete="given-name"
            value={firstName}
            onChange={(e) => setFirstName(e.target.value)}
            className={INPUT_CLASS}
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="details-last-name" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
            Last name
          </label>
          <input
            id="details-last-name"
            name="lastName"
            type="text"
            autoComplete="family-name"
            value={lastName}
            onChange={(e) => setLastName(e.target.value)}
            className={INPUT_CLASS}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label htmlFor="details-email" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
          Email
        </label>
        <input
          id="details-email"
          name="email"
          type="email"
          autoComplete="email"
          required
          aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.email)}
          aria-describedby={errors.email ? "details-email-error" : undefined}
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          className={INPUT_CLASS}
        />
        <FieldError message={errors.email} id="details-email-error" />
      </div>

      <SubmitButton
        loading={loading}
        label="Save Changes"
        loadingLabel="Saving…"
      />
    </form>
  );
}

/* ── Change password (via reset email) ───────────────────────── */

function ChangePasswordSection({ username }: { username: string }) {
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError("");
    setSuccess("");
    setLoading(true);

    // The core WPGraphQL updateUser mutation does not expose a password
    // field, so a reset link is sent instead. sendPasswordResetEmail
    // returns false on failure instead of throwing.
    const sent = await sendPasswordResetEmail(username);

    if (sent) {
      setSuccess(
        "A password reset link has been sent to your email. Follow it to set a new password.",
      );
    } else {
      setFormError("Could not send the reset link. Please try again.");
    }
    setLoading(false);
  }

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={handleSubmit}
      noValidate
      className="max-w-lg space-y-4 rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6] p-5"
    >
      <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase text-[#6c757d]">
        Change Password
      </h3>

      <p className="text-sm text-[#6c757d]">
        For security, password changes are handled through a reset link sent to
        your email.
      </p>

      {formError && (
        <div role="alert" className={ALERT_ERROR_CLASS}>
          {formError}
        </div>
      )}
      {success && (
        <div role="status" className={ALERT_SUCCESS_CLASS}>
          {success}
        </div>
      )}

      <SubmitButton
        loading={loading}
        label="Send Reset Link"
        loadingLabel="Sending…"
      />
    </form>
  );
}

// app/my-account/orders/[id]/order-detail-client.tsx
"use client";

import { use, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import AuthGuard from "@/components/auth-guard";
import AccountSkeleton from "@/components/account-skeleton";
import { getOrder, type OrderDetail } from "@/lib/auth";
import { GraphQLError } from "@/lib/graphql";
import { formatKD } from "@/lib/cart";
import { toAmount } from "@/lib/money";

const ALERT_ERROR_CLASS =
  "rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700";

const LINK_CLASS =
  "rounded text-sm font-medium text-[#e91e63] hover:text-[#c2185b] hover:underline focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]";

export default function OrderDetailClient({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  const { id } = use(params);
  return (
    <AuthGuard>
      <OrderDetailContent id={id} />
    </AuthGuard>
  );
}

function OrderDetailContent({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const [order, setOrder] = useState<OrderDetail | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    // Reset the loading state for the new order id.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    getOrder(id)
      .then((result) => {
        if (!cancelled) setOrder(result);
      })
      .catch((err: unknown) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setError(
          err instanceof GraphQLError ? err.message : "Could not load order.",
        );
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [id]);

  if (loading) return <AccountSkeleton />;

  if (error || !order) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <Link href="/my-account/orders" className={LINK_CLASS}>
          &larr; Back to orders
        </Link>
        <div role="alert" className={ALERT_ERROR_CLASS}>
          {error || "Order not found."}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const lineItems = order.lineItems?.nodes ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <Link
          href="/my-account/orders"
          className={`${LINK_CLASS} mb-4 inline-block`}
        >
          &larr; Back to orders
        </Link>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-[#212529]">
          Order #{order.orderNumber}
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#6c757d]">
          Placed on {new Date(order.date).toLocaleDateString()}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Summary */}
      <dl className="flex flex-wrap gap-6 rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6] p-4">
        <div>
          <dt className="text-xs font-medium uppercase text-[#6c757d]">
            Status
          </dt>
          <dd className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-medium capitalize text-[#212529]">
            {order.status}
          </dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt className="text-xs font-medium uppercase text-[#6c757d]">
            Total
          </dt>
          <dd className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-medium text-[#212529]">
            {formatKD(toAmount(order.total))}
          </dd>
        </div>
        {order.paymentMethodTitle && (
          <div>
            <dt className="text-xs font-medium uppercase text-[#6c757d]">
              Payment
            </dt>
            <dd className="mt-0.5 text-sm text-[#212529]">
              {order.paymentMethodTitle}
            </dd>
          </div>
        )}
      </dl>

      {/* Line items */}
      {lineItems.length > 0 && (
        <section aria-labelledby="order-items-heading">
          <h3
            id="order-items-heading"
            className="mb-3 text-lg font-semibold text-[#212529]"
          >
            Items
          </h3>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6]">
            <table className="min-w-full text-left text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-gray-50">
                <tr>
                  <th
                    scope="col"
                    className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]"
                  >
                    Product
                  </th>
                  <th
                    scope="col"
                    className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]"
                  >
                    Qty
                  </th>
                  <th
                    scope="col"
                    className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]"
                  >
                    Total
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#f1f5f9]">
                {lineItems.map((item, index) => (
                  <tr key={item.product?.id ?? `item-${index}`}>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[#212529]">
                      {item.product?.name || "—"}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[#6c757d]">
                      {item.quantity}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#212529]">
                      {formatKD(toAmount(item.total))}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* Addresses */}
      <div className="grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2">
        {order.billing && (
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6] p-4">
            <h4 className="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold uppercase text-[#6c757d]">
              Billing Address
            </h4>
            <address className="text-sm not-italic text-[#212529]">
              {order.billing.email && <p>{order.billing.email}</p>}
              {order.billing.phone && <p>{order.billing.phone}</p>}
              {order.billing.address1 && <p>{order.billing.address1}</p>}
              {order.billing.address2 && <p>{order.billing.address2}</p>}
              <p>
                {[
                  order.billing.city,
                  order.billing.state,
                  order.billing.postcode,
                ]
                  .filter(Boolean)
                  .join(", ")}
              </p>
              {order.billing.country && <p>{order.billing.country}</p>}
            </address>
          </div>
        )}
        {order.shipping && (
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6] p-4">
            <h4 className="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold uppercase text-[#6c757d]">
              Shipping Address
            </h4>
            <address className="text-sm not-italic text-[#212529]">
              {order.shipping.address1 && <p>{order.shipping.address1}</p>}
              {order.shipping.address2 && <p>{order.shipping.address2}</p>}
              <p>
                {[
                  order.shipping.city,
                  order.shipping.state,
                  order.shipping.postcode,
                ]
                  .filter(Boolean)
                  .join(", ")}
              </p>
              {order.shipping.country && <p>{order.shipping.country}</p>}
            </address>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

File: app/my-account/orders/page.tsx

// app/my-account/orders/page.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import AuthGuard from "@/components/auth-guard";
import AccountSkeleton from "@/components/account-skeleton";
import { getOrders, type Order } from "@/lib/auth";
import { GraphQLError } from "@/lib/graphql";
import { formatKD } from "@/lib/cart";
import { toAmount } from "@/lib/money";

const ALERT_ERROR_CLASS =
  "rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700";

const LINK_CLASS =
  "rounded text-sm font-medium text-[#e91e63] hover:text-[#c2185b] hover:underline focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]";

export default function OrdersPage() {
  return (
    <AuthGuard>
      <OrdersContent />
    </AuthGuard>
  );
}

function OrdersContent() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Order[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    getOrders()
      .then((result) => {
        if (!cancelled) setOrders(result);
      })
      .catch((err: unknown) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setError(
          err instanceof GraphQLError ? err.message : "Could not load orders.",
        );
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  if (loading) return <AccountSkeleton />;

  if (error) {
    return (
      <div role="alert" className={ALERT_ERROR_CLASS}>
        {error}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (orders.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6] px-6 py-12 text-center">
        <p className="text-[#6c757d]">You haven&apos;t placed any orders yet.</p>
        <Link href="/shop" className={`${LINK_CLASS} mt-3 inline-block`}>
          Browse products
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="orders-heading">
      <h2
        id="orders-heading"
        className="mb-4 text-xl font-semibold text-[#212529]"
      >
        Order History
      </h2>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6]">
        <table className="min-w-full text-left text-sm">
          <thead className="bg-gray-50">
            <tr>
              <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]">
                Order
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]">
                Date
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]">
                Status
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]">
                Total
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3">
                <span className="sr-only">Actions</span>
              </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#f1f5f9]">
            {orders.map((order) => (
              <tr key={order.id}>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#212529]">
                  #{order.orderNumber}
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[#6c757d]">
                  {new Date(order.date).toLocaleDateString()}
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <span className="inline-block rounded-full bg-gray-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium capitalize text-[#212529]">
                    {order.status}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#212529]">
                  {formatKD(toAmount(order.total))}
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                  <Link
                    href={`/my-account/orders/${order.databaseId}`}
                    className={LINK_CLASS}
                    aria-label={`View order ${order.orderNumber}`}
                  >
                    View
                  </Link>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

// app/my-account/reset-password/page.tsx
"use client";

import { useState, FormEvent, Suspense } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import SubmitButton from "@/components/submit-button";
import FieldError from "@/components/field-error";
import { resetUserPassword } from "@/lib/auth";
import { GraphQLError } from "@/lib/graphql";
import { validateResetPasswordForm } from "@/lib/validators";

export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-md animate-pulse space-y-4 py-8">
          <div className="h-6 w-2/3 rounded bg-gray-200" />
          <div className="h-4 w-full rounded bg-gray-200" />
        </div>
      }
    >
      <ResetPasswordContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

function ResetPasswordContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const key = searchParams.get("key");
  const login = searchParams.get("login");

  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  // Invalid or missing params
  if (!key || !login) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-md py-8 text-center">
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700"
        >
          Invalid reset link. Please request a new password reset.
        </div>
        <Link
          href="/my-account"
          className="mt-4 inline-block text-sm font-medium text-[#e91e63] hover:underline"
        >
          &larr; Back to My Account
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const validation = validateResetPasswordForm({ password, confirmPassword });
    if (!validation.valid) {
      setErrors(validation.errors);
      return;
    }
    setErrors({});
    setFormError("");
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      await resetUserPassword(key!, login!, password);
      setSuccess(true);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof GraphQLError) setFormError(err.message);
      else setFormError("Failed to reset password. The link may have expired.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  if (success) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-md py-8 text-center">
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="rounded-md border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-700"
        >
          Your password has been reset successfully.
        </div>
        <Link
          href="/my-account"
          className="mt-4 inline-block text-sm font-medium text-[#e91e63] hover:underline"
        >
          Log in with your new password
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-md py-8">
      <h2 className="mb-2 text-xl font-semibold text-[#212529]">
        Set New Password
      </h2>
      <p className="mb-6 text-sm text-[#6c757d]">
        Enter a new password for your account.
      </p>

      {formError && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="mb-4 rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700"
        >
          {formError}
        </div>
      )}

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="reset-password"
            className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-[#212529]"
          >
            New password
          </label>
          <input
            id="reset-password"
            type="password"
            autoComplete="new-password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded-md border border-[#dee2e6] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-[#212529] focus:border-[#e91e63] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#e91e63]"
          />
          <FieldError message={errors.password} id="reset-password-error" />
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#6c757d]">Minimum 8 characters.</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="reset-confirm-password"
            className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-[#212529]"
          >
            Confirm new password
          </label>
          <input
            id="reset-confirm-password"
            type="password"
            autoComplete="new-password"
            value={confirmPassword}
            onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full rounded-md border border-[#dee2e6] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-[#212529] focus:border-[#e91e63] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#e91e63]"
          />
          <FieldError
            message={errors.confirmPassword}
            id="reset-confirm-password-error"
          />
        </div>

        <SubmitButton loading={loading} label="Reset Password" />
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

// app/my-account/page.tsx
"use client";

import { Suspense, useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/use-auth";
import {
  getOrders,
  login,
  register,
  sendPasswordResetEmail,
  type Order,
  type User,
} from "@/lib/auth";
import { GraphQLError } from "@/lib/graphql";
import {
  validateForgotPasswordForm,
  validateLoginForm,
  validateRegisterForm,
} from "@/lib/validators";
import SubmitButton from "@/components/submit-button";
import FieldError from "@/components/field-error";
import AccountSkeleton from "@/components/account-skeleton";
import { formatKD } from "@/lib/cart";

/* ── Shared styles (design system) ───────────────────────────── */

const INPUT_CLASS =
  "w-full rounded-md border border-[#dee2e6] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-[#212529] placeholder:text-[#6c757d] focus:border-[#e91e63] focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]";

const LABEL_CLASS = "mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-[#212529]";

const ALERT_ERROR_CLASS =
  "rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700";

const ALERT_SUCCESS_CLASS =
  "rounded-md border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-700";

const LINK_CLASS =
  "text-sm font-medium text-[#e91e63] hover:text-[#c2185b] hover:underline focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] rounded";

/* ── Types ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

type AuthTab = "login" | "register" | "forgot";

const AUTH_TABS: ReadonlyArray<{ key: AuthTab; label: string }> = [
  { key: "login", label: "Login" },
  { key: "register", label: "Register" },
  { key: "forgot", label: "Forgot Password" },
];

/* ── Helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/**
 * Only allow same-site relative paths for the "return" parameter.
 * Blocks absolute URLs and protocol-relative URLs such as "//evil.com".
 */
function getSafeReturnPath(raw: string | null): string {
  if (!raw) return "/my-account";
  if (!raw.startsWith("/") || raw.startsWith("//") || raw.startsWith("/\\")) {
    return "/my-account";
  }
  return raw;
}

/** Shows GraphQL messages as-is (already cleaned), otherwise a fallback. */
function toUserMessage(err: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  return err instanceof GraphQLError ? err.message : fallback;
}

function isAuthTab(value: string | null): value is AuthTab {
  return value === "login" || value === "register" || value === "forgot";
}

/* ── Page (Suspense boundary for useSearchParams) ────────────── */

export default function MyAccountPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<AccountSkeleton />}>
      <MyAccountContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

function MyAccountContent() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  // Never show the login form while the session is being checked.
  if (loading) return <AccountSkeleton />;
  if (user) return <Dashboard user={user} />;
  return <AuthForms />;
}

/* ── Dashboard (signed-in users) ─────────────────────────────── */

function Dashboard({ user }: { user: User }) {
  const [recentOrders, setRecentOrders] = useState<Order[]>([]);
  const [ordersLoading, setOrdersLoading] = useState(true);
  const [ordersError, setOrdersError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    getOrders()
      .then((orders) => {
        if (!cancelled) setRecentOrders(orders.slice(0, 5));
      })
      .catch((err: unknown) => {
        // WooGraphQL may not be installed; show a friendly message instead.
        if (!cancelled) {
          setOrdersError(toUserMessage(err, "Could not load recent orders."));
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setOrdersLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  const quickLinks = [
    {
      href: "/my-account/orders",
      label: "Orders",
      desc: "View your order history",
    },
    {
      href: "/my-account/addresses",
      label: "Addresses",
      desc: "Edit billing and shipping",
    },
    {
      href: "/my-account/details",
      label: "Account Details",
      desc: "Name, email and password",
    },
  ] as const;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-[#212529]">
          Hello{user.firstName ? `, ${user.firstName}` : ""}!
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[#6c757d]">
          From your account dashboard you can view recent orders, manage your
          addresses, and edit your account details.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {quickLinks.map((card) => (
          <Link
            key={card.href}
            href={card.href}
            className="rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6] p-5 transition-shadow hover:shadow-md focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]"
          >
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-[#212529]">{card.label}</h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#6c757d]">{card.desc}</p>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>

      <section aria-labelledby="recent-orders-heading">
        <h3
          id="recent-orders-heading"
          className="mb-3 text-lg font-semibold text-[#212529]"
        >
          Recent Orders
        </h3>

        {ordersLoading ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-[#6c757d]">Loading orders…</p>
        ) : ordersError ? (
          <p role="alert" className={ALERT_ERROR_CLASS}>
            {ordersError}
          </p>
        ) : recentOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-[#6c757d]">No orders yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-[#dee2e6]">
            <table className="min-w-full text-left text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-gray-50">
                <tr>
                  <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]">
                    Order
                  </th>
                  <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]">
                    Date
                  </th>
                  <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]">
                    Status
                  </th>
                  <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#6c757d]">
                    Total
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#f1f5f9]">
                {recentOrders.map((order) => (
                  <tr key={order.id}>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <Link
                        href={`/my-account/orders/${order.databaseId}`}
                        className={LINK_CLASS}
                      >
                        #{order.orderNumber}
                      </Link>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[#6c757d]">
                      {new Date(order.date).toLocaleDateString()}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <span className="inline-block rounded-full bg-gray-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium capitalize text-[#212529]">
                        {order.status}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-[#212529]">
                      {formatKD(Number(order.total))}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Guest view: Login / Register / Forgot password ──────────── */

function AuthForms() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const requestedTab = searchParams.get("tab");
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<AuthTab>(
    isAuthTab(requestedTab) ? requestedTab : "login",
  );

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-md">
      <div
        className="mb-6 flex border-b border-[#dee2e6]"
        role="tablist"
        aria-label="Account access"
      >
        {AUTH_TABS.map(({ key, label }) => {
          const selected = tab === key;
          return (
            <button
              key={key}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              id={`auth-tab-${key}`}
              aria-selected={selected}
              aria-controls={`auth-panel-${key}`}
              tabIndex={selected ? 0 : -1}
              onClick={() => setTab(key)}
              className={`flex-1 border-b-2 px-3 py-3 text-[14px] font-medium transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] ${
                selected
                  ? "border-[#e91e63] text-[#e91e63]"
                  : "border-transparent text-[#6c757d] hover:text-[#212529]"
              }`}
            >
              {label}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div
        role="tabpanel"
        id={`auth-panel-${tab}`}
        aria-labelledby={`auth-tab-${tab}`}
      >
        {tab === "login" && <LoginForm />}
        {tab === "register" && <RegisterForm />}
        {tab === "forgot" && <ForgotPasswordForm />}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Login ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

function LoginForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    const validation = validateLoginForm({ username, password });
    setErrors(validation.errors);
    if (!validation.valid) return;

    setFormError("");
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await login(username, password);
      // AUTH_EVENT fires inside login(), so the header updates immediately.
      router.replace(getSafeReturnPath(searchParams.get("return")));
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setFormError(toUserMessage(err, "Login failed. Please try again."));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="space-y-4">
      {formError && (
        <div role="alert" className={ALERT_ERROR_CLASS}>
          {formError}
        </div>
      )}

      <div>
        <label htmlFor="login-username" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
          Username or email
        </label>
        <input
          id="login-username"
          name="username"
          type="text"
          autoComplete="username"
          required
          aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.username)}
          aria-describedby={errors.username ? "login-username-error" : undefined}
          value={username}
          onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
          className={INPUT_CLASS}
        />
        <FieldError message={errors.username} id="login-username-error" />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label htmlFor="login-password" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
          Password
        </label>
        <input
          id="login-password"
          name="password"
          type="password"
          autoComplete="current-password"
          required
          aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.password)}
          aria-describedby={errors.password ? "login-password-error" : undefined}
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          className={INPUT_CLASS}
        />
        <FieldError message={errors.password} id="login-password-error" />
      </div>

      <SubmitButton loading={loading} label="Log In" loadingLabel="Logging in…" />
    </form>
  );
}

/* ── Register ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

function RegisterForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    const validation = validateRegisterForm({ username, email, password });
    setErrors(validation.errors);
    if (!validation.valid) return;

    setFormError("");
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await register(username, email, password);
      router.replace(getSafeReturnPath(searchParams.get("return")));
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setFormError(toUserMessage(err, "Registration failed. Please try again."));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="space-y-4">
      {formError && (
        <div role="alert" className={ALERT_ERROR_CLASS}>
          {formError}
        </div>
      )}

      <div>
        <label htmlFor="reg-username" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
          Username
        </label>
        <input
          id="reg-username"
          name="username"
          type="text"
          autoComplete="username"
          required
          aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.username)}
          aria-describedby={errors.username ? "reg-username-error" : undefined}
          value={username}
          onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
          className={INPUT_CLASS}
        />
        <FieldError message={errors.username} id="reg-username-error" />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label htmlFor="reg-email" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
          Email
        </label>
        <input
          id="reg-email"
          name="email"
          type="email"
          autoComplete="email"
          required
          aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.email)}
          aria-describedby={errors.email ? "reg-email-error" : undefined}
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          className={INPUT_CLASS}
        />
        <FieldError message={errors.email} id="reg-email-error" />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label htmlFor="reg-password" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
          Password
        </label>
        <input
          id="reg-password"
          name="password"
          type="password"
          autoComplete="new-password"
          required
          minLength={8}
          aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.password)}
          aria-describedby={
            errors.password ? "reg-password-error reg-password-hint" : "reg-password-hint"
          }
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          className={INPUT_CLASS}
        />
        <p id="reg-password-hint" className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#6c757d]">
          Minimum 8 characters.
        </p>
        <FieldError message={errors.password} id="reg-password-error" />
      </div>

      <SubmitButton
        loading={loading}
        label="Register"
        loadingLabel="Creating account…"
      />
    </form>
  );
}

/* ── Forgot password ─────────────────────────────────────────── */

function ForgotPasswordForm() {
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    const validation = validateForgotPasswordForm({ username });
    setErrors(validation.errors);
    if (!validation.valid) return;

    setLoading(true);
    // sendPasswordResetEmail never reveals whether the account exists,
    // so the same confirmation is shown in every case.
    await sendPasswordResetEmail(username);
    setSubmitted(true);
    setLoading(false);
  }

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <p role="status" className={ALERT_SUCCESS_CLASS}>
        If an account with that username or email exists, a password reset link
        has been sent.
      </p>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="space-y-4">
      <p className="text-sm text-[#6c757d]">
        Enter your username or email and we will send you a reset link.
      </p>

      <div>
        <label htmlFor="forgot-username" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
          Username or email
        </label>
        <input
          id="forgot-username"
          name="username"
          type="text"
          autoComplete="username"
          required
          aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.username)}
          aria-describedby={errors.username ? "forgot-username-error" : undefined}
          value={username}
          onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
          className={INPUT_CLASS}
        />
        <FieldError message={errors.username} id="forgot-username-error" />
      </div>

      <SubmitButton
        loading={loading}
        label="Send Reset Link"
        loadingLabel="Sending…"
      />
    </form>
  );
}

File: app/order-confirmation/page.tsx
================
"use client";

import { Suspense, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import {
  CART_EVENT,
  type CartItem,
  clearCart,
  formatKD,
  getCartCount,
  getCartSubtotal,
  readCart,
} from "@/lib/cart";
import CheckoutSteps from "@/components/checkout-steps";

/* ============================================================
   CONSTANTS
   ============================================================ */
const LOGO_SRC: string = "/logo.png";

/* ============================================================
   ICONS
   ============================================================ */
function BasketIcon() {
  return (
    <svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M6 2 3 6v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h14a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V6l-3-4Z" />
      <path d="M3 6h18" />
      <path d="M16 10a4 4 0 0 1-8 0" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function CheckIcon() {
  return (

/* ============================================================
   CONFIRMATION CONTENT (reads the query string)
   ============================================================ */
function ConfirmationContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const orderNumber: string = (searchParams.get("order") ?? "").trim();

  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartItem[]>([]);

  /* Keep the header cart badge in sync. If the cart was not cleared
     earlier in the flow, clear it here (safe to call when already empty). */
  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = (): void => setCart(readCart());
    if (readCart().length > 0) {
      clearCart();
    }
    sync();
    window.addEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync);
    window.addEventListener("storage", sync);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync);
      window.removeEventListener("storage", sync);
    };
  }, []);

  const subtotal: number = useMemo(() => getCartSubtotal(cart), [cart]);
  const itemCount: number = useMemo(() => getCartCount(cart), [cart]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-white text-[#212529] antialiased">
      <Header cartCount={itemCount} cartTotal={subtotal} />

      <main>
        {/* Steps */}
        <section className="bg-[#f8f9fa] border-b border-[#f1f5f9]">
          <div className="max-w-[1260px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-0 py-8">
            <CheckoutSteps current={2} />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="bg-white py-8 sm:py-10">
          <div className="max-w-[1260px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-0">
            <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto text-center py-12" aria-live="polite">
              <span className="mx-auto w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-[#e8f5ee] text-[#198754] flex items-center justify-center">
                <CheckIcon />
              </span>
              <h1 className="mt-5 text-[24px] font-bold text-[#212529]">Thank you for your order</h1>

              {orderNumber ? (
                <p className="mt-2 text-[15px] text-[#6c757d]">
                  Your order number is{" "}
                  <span className="font-semibold text-[#212529] break-all">#{orderNumber}</span>.
                </p>
              ) : (
                <p className="mt-2 text-[15px] text-[#6c757d]">
                  Your order has been received. Your order number was not found in this link.
                </p>
              )}

              <p className="mt-2 text-[13px] text-[#6c757d]">
                Payment is cash on delivery. Please have the exact amount in KD ready when your order arrives.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center">
                <Link
                  href="/shop"
                  className="h-12 px-8 inline-flex items-center justify-center bg-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#c2185b] text-white text-[14px] font-semibold uppercase rounded-md transition-colors"
                >
                  Continue shopping
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

// app/orders/page.tsx
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { formatKD } from "@/lib/cart";
import type { TimelineStep, TrackedOrder } from "@/lib/orders";

/* ============================================================
   CONSTANTS
   ============================================================ */
const TRACK_API: string = "/api/orders/track";
const CLOSED_STATUSES: ReadonlyArray<string> = ["cancelled", "refunded", "failed"];

/* ============================================================
   HELPERS
   ============================================================ */
function formatDate(value: string): string {
  const date: Date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;
  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "2-digit", month: "short", year: "numeric" });
}

function statusBadgeClass(status: string): string {
  if (status === "completed") return "bg-[#e8f5ee] text-[#198754]";
  if (CLOSED_STATUSES.includes(status)) return "bg-[#f8d7da] text-[#842029]";
  return "bg-[#fde4ec] text-[#e91e63]";
}

function inputClass(hasError: boolean): string {
  return `w-full h-12 px-4 bg-white border rounded-md text-[15px] text-[#212529] placeholder:text-[#adb5bd] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#e91e63]/20 focus:border-[#e91e63] transition-colors ${
    hasError ? "border-[#dc3545]" : "border-[#dee2e6]"
  }`;
}

/* ============================================================
   ICONS
   ============================================================ */
function CheckIcon(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5" />
    </svg>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   SUB COMPONENTS
   ============================================================ */
function FieldLabel({ htmlFor, children }: { htmlFor: string; children: ReactNode }): ReactNode {
  return (
    <label htmlFor={htmlFor} className="block text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#212529] mb-1.5">
      {children} <span className="text-[#e91e63]">*</span>
    </label>
  );
}

function Timeline({ steps }: { steps: TimelineStep[] }): ReactNode {
  return (
    <ol className="mt-6 space-y-5" aria-label="Order progress">
      {steps.map((step) => {
        const active: boolean = step.done || step.current;
        return (
          <li key={step.key} className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <span
              className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                active ? "bg-[#e91e63] text-white" : "bg-[#f1f5f9] text-[#adb5bd]"
              }`}
              aria-hidden="true"
            >
              {step.done ? <CheckIcon /> : step.current ? <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white" /> : null}
            </span>
            <span
              className={`text-[15px] ${
                step.current ? "font-bold text-[#e91e63]" : step.done ? "font-medium text-[#212529]" : "text-[#6c757d]"
              }`}
            >
              {step.label}
            </span>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ol>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   PAGE
   Header and footer come from app/layout.tsx (SiteHeader / SiteFooter).
   ============================================================ */
export default function OrdersTrackingPage(): ReactNode {
  const [orderNumber, setOrderNumber] = useState<string>("");
  const [contact, setContact] = useState<string>("");
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<{ orderNumber?: string; contact?: string }>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [order, setOrder] = useState<TrackedOrder | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>): Promise<void> => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (loading) return;

    const nextErrors: { orderNumber?: string; contact?: string } = {};
    if (!/^\d{1,12}$/.test(orderNumber.trim().replace(/^#/, ""))) {
      nextErrors.orderNumber = "Enter the order number from your confirmation (digits only).";
    }
    if (contact.trim().length < 5) {
      nextErrors.contact = "Enter the email or mobile number used at checkout.";
    }

    setErrors(nextErrors);
    setError(null);
    setOrder(null);

    if (Object.keys(nextErrors).length > 0) {
      const firstId: string = nextErrors.orderNumber ? "orderNumber" : "contact";
      document.getElementById(firstId)?.focus();
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(TRACK_API, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ orderNumber: orderNumber.trim(), contact: contact.trim() }),
      });

      const data: unknown = await res.json().catch(() => null);

      if (!res.ok) {
        const message: string =
          typeof data === "object" && data !== null && "error" in data
            ? String((data as { error: unknown }).error)
            : "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
        throw new Error(message);
      }

      setOrder(data as TrackedOrder);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Something went wrong. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const subtotal: number = order ? Number(order.total) - Number(order.shippingTotal) : 0;

  return (
    <>
      {/* Title and breadcrumb */}
      <section className="bg-[#f8f9fa] border-b border-[#f1f5f9]">
        <div className="max-w-[1260px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-0 py-8 text-center">
          <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight">Track your order</h1>
          <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="mt-3 text-sm text-[#6c757d]">
            <Link href="/" className="hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors">Home</Link>
            <span className="mx-2">/</span>
            <span className="text-[#212529]">Track order</span>
          </nav>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="bg-white py-10 sm:py-12">
        <div className="max-w-[640px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6">
          {/* Lookup form */}
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="bg-white border border-[#f1f5f9] rounded-lg p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-[0_2px_12px_rgba(33,37,41,0.04)] space-y-5">
            <p className="text-[14px] text-[#6c757d]">
              Enter your order number and the email or mobile number you used at checkout.
            </p>

            <div>
              <FieldLabel htmlFor="orderNumber">Order number</FieldLabel>
              <input
                id="orderNumber"
                name="orderNumber"
                inputMode="numeric"
                autoComplete="off"
                placeholder="e.g. 1042"
                value={orderNumber}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setOrderNumber(e.target.value);
                  if (errors.orderNumber) setErrors((p) => ({ ...p, orderNumber: undefined }));
                }}
                aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.orderNumber)}
                aria-describedby={errors.orderNumber ? "orderNumber-error" : undefined}
                className={inputClass(Boolean(errors.orderNumber))}
              />
              {errors.orderNumber && (
                <p id="orderNumber-error" className="mt-1.5 text-[12px] text-[#dc3545]">{errors.orderNumber}</p>
              )}
            </div>

            <div>
              <FieldLabel htmlFor="contact">Email or mobile number</FieldLabel>
              <input
                id="contact"
                name="contact"
                autoComplete="off"
                placeholder="you@example.com or 5XXX XXXX"
                value={contact}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setContact(e.target.value);
                  if (errors.contact) setErrors((p) => ({ ...p, contact: undefined }));
                }}
                aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.contact)}
                aria-describedby={errors.contact ? "contact-error" : undefined}
                className={inputClass(Boolean(errors.contact))}
              />
              {errors.contact && (
                <p id="contact-error" className="mt-1.5 text-[12px] text-[#dc3545]">{errors.contact}</p>
              )}
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full h-[52px] bg-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#c2185b] disabled:opacity-70 disabled:cursor-wait text-white text-[14px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide rounded-md transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
            >
              {loading ? "Checking..." : "Track order"}
            </button>
          </form>

          {error && (
            <div role="alert" className="mt-5 border border-[#f5c2c7] bg-[#f8d7da] text-[#842029] text-[14px] p-4 rounded-md">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Result */}
          {order && (
            <section className="mt-8 bg-white border border-[#f1f5f9] rounded-lg p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-[0_2px_12px_rgba(33,37,41,0.04)]" aria-live="polite">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-[18px] font-bold">Order #{order.orderNumber}</h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6c757d]">Placed on {formatDate(order.dateCreated)}</p>
                </div>
                <span className={`px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-bold uppercase rounded-full ${statusBadgeClass(order.status)}`}>
                  {order.statusLabel}
                </span>
              </div>

              {!CLOSED_STATUSES.includes(order.status) && <Timeline steps={order.timeline} />}

              {order.trackingNumber && (
                <div className="mt-6 p-4 bg-[#f8f9fa] border border-[#f1f5f9] rounded-md text-[14px] space-y-1">
                  <p><span className="font-semibold">Courier:</span> {order.carrier ?? "Courier"}</p>
                  <p><span className="font-semibold">Tracking number:</span> {order.trackingNumber}</p>
                  {order.trackingUrl && (
                    <a
                      href={order.trackingUrl}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      className="inline-block mt-2 text-[#e91e63] underline underline-offset-2"
                    >
                      Track with courier
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="mt-6 border-t border-[#e9ecef] pt-4">
                <h3 className="text-[14px] font-bold mb-2">Items</h3>
                <ul className="divide-y divide-[#e9ecef] text-[14px]">
                  {order.items.map((item, index) => (
                    <li key={`${item.name}-${index}`} className="py-2.5 flex justify-between gap-4">
                      <span className="min-w-0">
                        {item.name} <span className="text-[#6c757d]">× {item.quantity}</span>
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">{formatKD(Number(item.total))}</span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>

                <dl className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-[14px]">
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <dt className="text-[#6c757d]">Subtotal</dt>
                    <dd className="font-semibold">{formatKD(subtotal)}</dd>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <dt className="text-[#6c757d]">Delivery</dt>
                    <dd className="font-semibold">{formatKD(Number(order.shippingTotal))}</dd>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between pt-3 border-t border-[#dee2e6]">
                    <dt className="font-bold">Total</dt>
                    <dd className="text-[18px] font-bold text-[#e91e63]">{formatKD(Number(order.total))}</dd>
                  </div>
                </dl>
              </div>

              <p className="mt-5 text-[12px] text-[#6c757d]">
                Payment: Cash on delivery. Please keep the exact amount ready when your order arrives.
              </p>
            </section>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

// app/product/[slug]/page.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, useSyncExternalStore, type ReactNode } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { addToCart } from "@/lib/cart";
import { WISHLIST_EVENT, readWishlist, toggleWishlist } from "@/lib/wishlist";

/* ============================================================
   TYPES
   ============================================================ */
interface GraphQLProduct {
  id: string;
  databaseId: number;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  description: string | null;
  shortDescription: string | null;
  price: string;
  regularPrice: string | null;
  image: { sourceUrl: string } | null;
  productCategories: { nodes: Array<{ name: string; slug: string }> } | null;
  productTags: { nodes: Array<{ name: string; slug: string }> } | null;
}

interface ProductResponse {
  data?: { product?: GraphQLProduct | null };
  errors?: Array<{ message: string }>;
}

interface ProductsResponse {
  data?: { products?: { nodes: GraphQLProduct[] } };
  errors?: Array<{ message: string }>;
}

interface InfoItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  content: ReactNode;
}

/* ============================================================
   CONSTANTS
   ============================================================ */
const API_URL: string =
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WORDPRESS_API_URL || "http://localhost/shophope/graphql";

const PRODUCT_QUERY: string = `
  query GetProduct($slug: ID!) {
    product(id: $slug, idType: SLUG) {
      id
      databaseId
      name
      slug
      description
      shortDescription
      ... on SimpleProduct {
        price
        regularPrice
      }
      image {
        sourceUrl
      }
      productCategories {
        nodes {
          name
          slug
        }
      }
      productTags {
        nodes {
          name
          slug
        }
      }
    }
  }
`;

const RELATED_QUERY: string = `
