// app/wishlist/page.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { addToCart } from "@/lib/cart";
import { WISHLIST_EVENT, readWishlist, writeWishlist, type WishlistItem } from "@/lib/wishlist";

/* ============================================================
   CONSTANTS
   ============================================================ */
const CURRENCY: string = "د.ك";

/* ============================================================
   HELPERS
   ============================================================ */
function formatPrice(amount: number): string {
  return `${CURRENCY} ${amount.toFixed(2)}`;
}

/* ============================================================
   ICONS
   ============================================================ */
function TrashIcon(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M3 6h18" />
      <path d="M8 6V4h8v2" />
      <path d="M19 6l-1 14H6L5 6" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function EmptyHeartIcon(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="72" height="72" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#e91e63" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M20.8 4.6a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.8 0L12 5.7l-1-1.1a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.8 7.8l1 1.1L12 21l7.8-7.5 1-1.1a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0-7.8Z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   WISHLIST ROW
   ============================================================ */
function WishlistRow({
  item,
  onAddToCart,
  onRemove,
}: {
  item: WishlistItem;
  onAddToCart: (item: WishlistItem) => void;
  onRemove: (id: string) => void;
}): ReactNode {
  const [imgFailed, setImgFailed] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const showImage: boolean = Boolean(item.image) && !imgFailed;

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-[72px_1fr] md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,2fr)_1fr_200px_40px] gap-4 md:gap-6 items-center py-6 border-b border-[#f1f5f9]">
      {/* Product */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 min-w-0">
        <Link
          href={`/product/${item.slug}`}
          className="block w-[72px] h-[72px] shrink-0 bg-[#f8f9fa] border border-[#f1f5f9] overflow-hidden"
        >
          {showImage ? (
            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
            <img
              src={item.image ?? undefined}
              alt={item.name}
              onError={() => setImgFailed(true)}
              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center">
              <svg viewBox="0 0 200 150" className="max-h-[40px] w-auto" role="img" aria-label="Product image placeholder">
                <rect x="62" y="40" width="76" height="76" rx="14" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#212529" strokeWidth="5" />
                <rect x="76" y="22" width="48" height="20" rx="6" fill="#e91e63" />
                <circle cx="100" cy="80" r="16" fill="#fde4ec" />
              </svg>
            </div>
          )}
        </Link>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <Link
            href={`/product/${item.slug}`}
            className="block text-[15px] font-medium text-[#212529] hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors line-clamp-2"
          >
            {item.name}
          </Link>
          {/* Mobile: price shown here */}
          <p className="md:hidden mt-1 text-[14px] font-bold text-[#e91e63]">{formatPrice(item.price)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Price (desktop) */}
      <div className="hidden md:block text-[15px] font-bold text-[#e91e63]">{formatPrice(item.price)}</div>

      {/* Actions */}
      <div className="col-span-2 md:col-span-1 flex items-center gap-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onAddToCart(item)}
          className="flex-1 md:flex-none h-[42px] px-5 bg-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#c2185b] active:scale-[0.98] text-white text-[13px] font-semibold uppercase rounded-md transition-all"
        >
          Add to cart
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onRemove(item.id)}
          className="md:hidden inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-[#6c757d] hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors"
        >
          <TrashIcon />
          Remove
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Remove (desktop) */}
      <div className="hidden md:flex justify-end">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onRemove(item.id)}
          aria-label={`Remove ${item.name} from wishlist`}
          className="text-[#6c757d] hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors"
        >
          <TrashIcon />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   PAGE
   Header and footer come from app/layout.tsx (SiteHeader / SiteFooter).
   ============================================================ */
export default function WishlistPage(): ReactNode {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<WishlistItem[]>([]);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);

  /* Load wishlist and keep it in sync */
  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = (): void => setItems(readWishlist());
    sync();
    // Hydrate from localStorage after mount to avoid an SSR mismatch.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    setLoaded(true);
    window.addEventListener(WISHLIST_EVENT, sync);
    window.addEventListener("storage", sync);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(WISHLIST_EVENT, sync);
      window.removeEventListener("storage", sync);
    };
  }, []);

  /* Toast auto-hide */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2200);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast]);

  /* Adds to the shared cart. The header updates itself via CART_EVENT. */
  const handleAddToCart = (item: WishlistItem): void => {
    addToCart({ id: item.id, slug: item.slug, name: item.name, image: item.image, price: item.price }, 1);
    setToast(`Added to cart: ${item.name}`);
  };

  const handleRemove = (id: string): void => {
    writeWishlist(readWishlist().filter((i) => i.id !== id));
  };

  const handleClear = (): void => {
    writeWishlist([]);
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* Breadcrumb / title */}
      <section className="bg-[#f8f9fa] border-b border-[#f1f5f9]">
        <div className="max-w-[1260px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-0 py-6">
          <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-[#6c757d]">
            <Link href="/" className="hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors">Home</Link>
            <span className="mx-2">/</span>
            <span className="text-[#212529]">Wishlist</span>
          </nav>
          <h1 className="mt-2 text-[28px] sm:text-[32px] font-semibold leading-tight">Wishlist</h1>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="bg-white py-8 lg:py-12">
        <div className="max-w-[1260px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-0">
          {/* Loading */}
          {!loaded && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="h-24 bg-[#f8f9fa] animate-pulse" />
              <div className="h-24 bg-[#f8f9fa] animate-pulse" />
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Empty */}
          {loaded && items.length === 0 && (
            <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto text-center py-16 border border-[#f1f5f9] bg-[#f8f9fa] px-6">
              <div className="flex justify-center mb-5">
                <EmptyHeartIcon />
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-[20px] font-semibold">This wishlist is empty</h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-[14px] text-[#6c757d]">
                You don&apos;t have any products in the wishlist yet. You will find a lot of interesting products on our
                Shop page.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/shop"
                className="mt-6 inline-flex h-[48px] px-8 items-center justify-center bg-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#c2185b] text-white text-[14px] font-semibold uppercase rounded-md transition-colors"
              >
                Return to shop
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* List */}
          {loaded && items.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <div className="hidden md:grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,2fr)_1fr_200px_40px] gap-6 pb-3 border-b-2 border-[#212529] text-[13px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#6c757d]">
                <span>Product</span>
                <span>Price</span>
                <span>Action</span>
                <span />
              </div>

              <div>
                {items.map((item) => (
                  <WishlistRow key={item.id} item={item} onAddToCart={handleAddToCart} onRemove={handleRemove} />
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <Link
                  href="/shop"
                  className="inline-flex h-[44px] px-6 items-center border border-[#dee2e6] rounded-md text-[14px] font-medium hover:border-[#e91e63] hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors"
                >
                  ← Return to shop
                </Link>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleClear}
                  className="text-[14px] font-medium text-[#6c757d] hover:text-[#e91e63] underline underline-offset-4 transition-colors"
                >
                  Clear wishlist
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Toast */}
      <div
        aria-live="polite"
        className={`fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-[60] max-w-[280px] bg-[#212529] text-white text-sm font-medium px-5 py-3 rounded-lg shadow-xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-all duration-300 ${
          toast ? "translate-y-0 opacity-100" : "translate-y-5 opacity-0 pointer-events-none"
        }`}
      >
        <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#e91e63] shrink-0" />
        <span className="line-clamp-1">{toast ?? ""}</span>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   HELPERS
   ============================================================ */

/** WooCommerce prices come as formatted HTML. Convert them to plain text. */
function htmlToText(html: string): string {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return html.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, "");
  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, "text/html");
  return doc.body.textContent ?? "";
}

/** Extract the numeric amount from a formatted price string. */
function parseAmount(html: string | null | undefined): number | null {
  if (!html) return null;
  const match = htmlToText(html).match(/\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?/);
  if (!match) return null;
  const value = parseFloat(match[0].replace(",", "."));
  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}

/** Discount percentage from real price vs regular price. Returns null if not on sale. */
function getDiscountPercent(price: string | null, regularPrice: string | null): number | null {
  const current = parseAmount(price);
  const regular = parseAmount(regularPrice);
  if (current === null || regular === null || regular <= current) return null;
  return Math.round(((regular - current) / regular) * 100);
}

/* ============================================================
   ICONS (inline SVG)
   ============================================================ */
function HeartIcon({ filled = false }: { filled?: boolean }): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill={filled ? "currentColor" : "none"} stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M20.8 4.6a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.8 0L12 5.7l-1-1.1a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.8 7.8l1 1.1L12 21l7.8-7.5 1-1.1a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0-7.8Z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function ChevronIcon({ direction }: { direction: "left" | "right" }): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      {direction === "left" ? <path d="m15 18-6-6 6-6" /> : <path d="m9 18 6-6-6-6" />}
    </svg>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   HERO CAROUSEL  (desktop height 460px)
   Slides whose image fails to load are skipped automatically.
   ============================================================ */
function HeroCarousel(): ReactNode {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState<number>(0);
  const [failedIds, setFailedIds] = useState<number[]>([]);

  const slides: HeroSlide[] = HERO_SLIDES.filter((s) => !failedIds.includes(s.id));

  /* Derive a valid index during render, so no effect is needed to correct it */
  const activeIndex: number = slides.length > 0 ? index % slides.length : 0;

  /* Autoplay */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (slides.length < 2) return;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      setIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % slides.length);
    }, 5000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [slides.length]);

  const goTo = (target: number): void => {
    if (slides.length === 0) return;
    setIndex((target + slides.length) % slides.length);
  };

  const markFailed = (id: number): void => {
    setFailedIds((prev) => (prev.includes(id) ? prev : [...prev, id]));
  };

  if (slides.length === 0) {
    return <section className="w-full h-[220px] sm:h-[300px] lg:h-[460px] bg-[#f8f9fa]" aria-hidden="true" />;
  }

  return (
    <section
      className="relative w-full p-0 m-0 overflow-hidden bg-white h-[220px] sm:h-[300px] lg:h-[460px]"
      aria-roledescription="carousel"
      aria-label="Promotions"
    >
      <div
        className="flex h-full transition-transform duration-700 ease-in-out"
        style={{ transform: `translateX(-${activeIndex * 100}%)` }}
      >
        {slides.map((slide) => (
          <div key={slide.id} className="relative min-w-full h-full">
            {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
            <img
              src={slide.src}
              alt={slide.alt}
              onError={() => markFailed(slide.id)}
              className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover object-center select-none"
              draggable={false}
            />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Invisible link over the whole hero, routes to /shop */}
      <Link href="/shop" aria-label="Shop collection" className="absolute inset-0 z-10 cursor-pointer">
        <span className="sr-only">Shop collection</span>
      </Link>

      {slides.length > 1 && (
        <>
          {/* Arrows (above overlay) */}
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Previous slide"
            onClick={() => goTo(activeIndex - 1)}
            className="hidden sm:flex absolute z-20 left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-white/90 text-[#212529] shadow items-center justify-center transition-colors hover:bg-[#e91e63] hover:text-white focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]"
          >
            <ChevronIcon direction="left" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Next slide"
            onClick={() => goTo(activeIndex + 1)}
            className="hidden sm:flex absolute z-20 right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-white/90 text-[#212529] shadow items-center justify-center transition-colors hover:bg-[#e91e63] hover:text-white focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]"
          >
            <ChevronIcon direction="right" />
          </button>

          {/* Dots (above overlay) */}
          <div className="absolute z-20 bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 flex gap-2">
            {slides.map((slide, i) => (
              <button
                key={slide.id}
                type="button"
                aria-label={`Go to slide ${i + 1}`}
                aria-current={i === activeIndex}
                onClick={() => goTo(i)}
                className={`h-2.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] ${
                  i === activeIndex ? "w-7 bg-[#e91e63]" : "w-2.5 bg-white/80 hover:bg-white"
                }`}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

   HOT LIST

function HotList(): ReactNode {

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4 lg:gap-[13px]">
          {HOT_CARDS.map((card) => (
            <div
              key={card.id}
              className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-none h-[228.75px] w-full lg:w-[305px]"
              style={{ backgroundColor: card.bg }}
            >
              {/* Product photo: right side, full card height */}
              {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
              <img
                src={card.src}
                alt=""
                aria-hidden="true"
                className="absolute right-0 top-0 h-full w-[62%] object-cover object-center select-none pointer-events-none"
              />

              {/* Text, left side */}
              <div className="relative z-10 h-full pl-[38px] pr-4 pt-[48px] flex flex-col items-start">
                <span className="text-[18px] leading-none text-[#9a9a9a]">{card.eyebrow}</span>
                <h3 className="mt-3 text-[30px] leading-[1.17] font-normal text-[#212529]">
                  {card.title.map((line) => (
                    <span key={line} className="block whitespace-nowrap">
                      {line}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </h3>
                <Link
                  href={card.path}
                  className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center justify-center h-[52px] px-6 bg-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#c2185b] text-white text-[15px] font-semibold uppercase rounded-full transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
                >
                  SEE MORE
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   PRODUCT CARD
   Card and photo are square (no radius).
   ADD TO CART button has a slight radius.
   Heart button sits on the top-right of the photo.
   ============================================================ */
interface ProductCardProps {
  product: GraphQLProduct;
  isWishlisted: boolean;
  onToggleWishlist: (product: GraphQLProduct) => void;
  onAdd: (product: GraphQLProduct) => void;
}

function ProductCard({ product, isWishlisted, onToggleWishlist, onAdd }: ProductCardProps): ReactNode {
  const [imgFailed, setImgFailed] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const categoryName: string = product.productCategories?.nodes?.[0]?.name ?? "Uncategorized";
  const discount: number | null = getDiscountPercent(product.price, product.regularPrice);
  const showImage: boolean = Boolean(product.image?.sourceUrl) && !imgFailed;
  const productHref: string = `/product/${product.slug}`;

  return (
    <article className="group relative flex flex-col h-full bg-white rounded-none border border-[#f1f5f9] overflow-hidden shadow-[0_2px_12px_rgba(33,37,41,0.06)] hover:shadow-[0_10px_30px_rgba(33,37,41,0.12)] transition-shadow duration-300">
      {discount !== null && (
        <span className="absolute top-3 left-3 z-10 bg-[#e91e63] text-white text-[13px] font-bold w-[56px] h-[56px] rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
          {`-${discount}%`}
        </span>
      )}

      {/* Photo wrapper */}
      <div className="relative p-3 pb-0">
        <Link
          href={productHref}
          aria-label={product.name}
          className="block w-full aspect-square rounded-none overflow-hidden bg-[#f8f9fa]"
        >
          {showImage ? (
            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
            <img
              src={product.image?.sourceUrl}
              alt={product.name}
              loading="lazy"
              onError={() => setImgFailed(true)}
              className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center">
              <svg viewBox="0 0 200 150" className="max-h-[160px] w-auto" role="img" aria-label="Product image placeholder">
                <rect x="62" y="40" width="76" height="76" rx="14" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#212529" strokeWidth="5" />
                <rect x="76" y="22" width="48" height="20" rx="6" fill="#e91e63" />
                <circle cx="100" cy="80" r="16" fill="#fde4ec" />
              </svg>
            </div>
          )}
        </Link>

        {/* Wishlist heart */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onToggleWishlist(product)}
          aria-pressed={isWishlisted}
          aria-label={isWishlisted ? "Remove from wishlist" : "Add to wishlist"}
          className={`absolute top-5 right-5 z-10 w-[40px] h-[40px] inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full shadow transition-colors ${
            isWishlisted ? "bg-[#e91e63] text-white" : "bg-white text-[#212529] hover:text-[#e91e63]"
          }`}
        >
          <HeartIcon filled={isWishlisted} />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 p-4">
        <Link
          href={productHref}
          title={product.name}
          className="block truncate text-[16px] font-normal text-[#212529] hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors"
        >
          {product.name}
        </Link>

        <p className="mt-2 text-[14px] text-[#6c757d] line-clamp-2">{categoryName}</p>

        <div className="mt-auto pt-3 flex flex-col gap-3">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-2">
            {product.price ? (
              <span className="text-[16px] font-bold text-[#e91e63]" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: product.price }} />
            ) : (
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#6c757d]">Select options</span>
            )}
            {discount !== null && product.regularPrice && (
              <span className="text-[14px] text-[#6c757d] line-through" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: product.regularPrice }} />
            )}
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onAdd(product)}
            disabled={!product.price}
            className="w-full h-[52px] bg-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#c2185b] disabled:bg-[#f1f5f9] disabled:text-[#6c757d] active:scale-[0.98] text-white text-[15px] font-semibold uppercase rounded-md transition-all focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
          >
            ADD TO CART
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   FEATURED PRODUCTS SECTION
   ============================================================ */
interface FeaturedProductsProps {
  products: GraphQLProduct[];
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  wishlist: string[];
  onRetry: () => void;
  onAdd: (product: GraphQLProduct) => void;
  onToggleWishlist: (product: GraphQLProduct) => void;
}

function FeaturedProducts({
  products,
  loading,
  error,
  wishlist,
  onRetry,
  onAdd,
  onToggleWishlist,
}: FeaturedProductsProps): ReactNode {
  return (
    <section className="bg-white py-6 sm:py-8" aria-labelledby="featured-title">
      <div className="max-w-[1260px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-0">
        <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-8">
          <p className="text-[16px] uppercase text-[#6c757d]">Welcome to Shop &amp; Hope</p>
          <h2 id="featured-title" className="mt-3 text-3xl sm:text-4xl lg:text-[44px] font-bold text-[#212529] tracking-tight">
            Featured Products
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-4 text-sm sm:text-base leading-relaxed text-[#6c757d]">
            Feel confident and stylish in any outfit with Shop &amp; Hope. With a wide
            <br className="hidden sm:block" />
            range of options for men, women, and children.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div>
          {loading && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6" aria-busy="true">
              {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="h-[520px] bg-[#f8f9fa] border border-[#f1f5f9] animate-pulse" />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && error && (
            <div role="alert" className="max-w-xl mx-auto text-center border border-[#f1f5f9] p-8 bg-[#f8f9fa]">
              <p className="font-bold text-[#212529]">We couldn&apos;t load products.</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#6c757d]">{error}</p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onRetry}
                className="mt-5 bg-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#c2185b] text-white text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest px-6 py-3 rounded-full transition-colors"
              >
                RETRY
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && !error && products.length === 0 && (
            <p className="text-center text-[#6c757d]">No products found.</p>
          )}

          {!loading && !error && products.length > 0 && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
              {products.map((product) => (
                <ProductCard
                  key={product.id}
                  product={product}
                  isWishlisted={wishlist.includes(product.id)}
                  onToggleWishlist={onToggleWishlist}
                  onAdd={onAdd}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   PAGE
   Header and footer come from app/layout.tsx (SiteHeader / SiteFooter).
   ============================================================ */
export default function HomePage(): ReactNode {
  const [products, setProducts] = useState<GraphQLProduct[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState<number>(0);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [wishlist, setWishlist] = useState<string[]>([]);

  /* Keep card hearts in sync with localStorage wishlist */
  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = (): void => setWishlist(readWishlist().map((i) => i.id));
    sync();
    window.addEventListener(WISHLIST_EVENT, sync);
    window.addEventListener("storage", sync);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(WISHLIST_EVENT, sync);
      window.removeEventListener("storage", sync);
    };
  }, []);

  /* Fetch 8 products from WPGraphQL */
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function loadProducts(): Promise<void> {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError(null);

        const res = await fetch(API_URL, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ query: PRODUCTS_QUERY }),
          signal: controller.signal,
        });

        if (!res.ok) {
          throw new Error(`GraphQL request failed with status ${res.status}`);
        }

        const json: GraphQLResponse = await res.json();

        if (json.errors && json.errors.length > 0) {
          throw new Error(json.errors[0].message);
        }

        setProducts(json.data?.products?.nodes ?? []);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof DOMException && err.name === "AbortError") return;
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to load products.");
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    loadProducts();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [reloadKey]);

  /* Auto-hide toast */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2200);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast]);

  /* Save the product to the shared cart. The header updates itself via CART_EVENT. */
  const handleAddToCart = (product: GraphQLProduct): void => {
    addToCart({
      id: String(product.databaseId),
      slug: product.slug,
      name: product.name,
      image: product.image?.sourceUrl ?? null,
      price: parseAmount(product.price) ?? 0,
    });
    setToast(`Added: ${product.name}`);
  };

  /* Save the full product to localStorage so the wishlist page can show it */
  const handleToggleWishlist = (product: GraphQLProduct): void => {
    const added: boolean = toggleWishlist({
      id: product.id,
      slug: product.slug,
      name: product.name,
      image: product.image?.sourceUrl ?? null,
      price: parseAmount(product.price) ?? 0,
    });
    setToast(added ? "Added to wishlist" : "Removed from wishlist");
  };

  return (
    <>
      <HeroCarousel />
      <HotList />
      <FeaturedProducts
        products={products}
        loading={loading}
        error={error}
        wishlist={wishlist}
        onRetry={() => setReloadKey((k) => k + 1)}
        onAdd={handleAddToCart}
        onToggleWishlist={handleToggleWishlist}
      />
      <TrendingSection />
      <VideoSection />

      {/* Toast */}
      <div
        aria-live="polite"
        className={`fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-[60] max-w-[280px] bg-[#212529] text-white text-sm font-medium px-5 py-3 rounded-lg shadow-xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-all duration-300 ${
          toast ? "translate-y-0 opacity-100" : "translate-y-5 opacity-0 pointer-events-none"
        }`}
      >
        <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#e91e63] shrink-0" />
        <span className="line-clamp-1">{toast ?? ""}</span>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

// components/account-sidebar.tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { logout } from "@/lib/auth";

type SidebarVariant = "vertical" | "horizontal";

const links = [
  { href: "/my-account", label: "Dashboard", icon: "📋" },
  { href: "/my-account/orders", label: "Orders", icon: "📦" },
  { href: "/my-account/addresses", label: "Addresses", icon: "📍" },
  { href: "/my-account/details", label: "Account Details", icon: "👤" },
  { href: "/wishlist", label: "Wishlist", icon: "❤️" },
] as const;

function SidebarInner({ variant }: { variant: SidebarVariant }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const isHorizontal = variant === "horizontal";

  async function handleLogout() {
    await logout();
    router.push("/my-account");
  }

  const itemBase =
    "flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-[14px] font-medium transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]";

  return (
    <nav aria-label="Account navigation">
      <ul
        className={
          isHorizontal
            ? "flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2"
            : "space-y-1"
        }
      >
        {links.map((link) => {
          const active = pathname === link.href;
          return (
            <li key={link.href} className={isHorizontal ? "shrink-0" : undefined}>
              <Link
                href={link.href}
                aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                className={`${itemBase} ${
                  active
                    ? "bg-[#e91e63]/10 text-[#e91e63]"
                    : "text-[#212529] hover:bg-gray-100"
                }`}
              >
                <span aria-hidden="true">{link.icon}</span>
                {link.label}
              </Link>
            </li>
          );
        })}
        <li className={isHorizontal ? "shrink-0" : undefined}>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className={`${itemBase} w-full text-[#6c757d] hover:bg-gray-100`}
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">🚪</span>
            Logout
          </button>
        </li>
      </ul>
    </nav>
  );
}

export default function AccountSidebar({
  variant = "vertical",
}: {
  variant?: SidebarVariant;
}) {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={null}>
      <SidebarInner variant={variant} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

// components/auth-guard.tsx
"use client";
// ------------------------------------------------------------------
// Wraps protected pages. While the session is checked a skeleton is
// shown. If the user is not signed in they are redirected to
// /my-account with a return path.
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/use-auth";
import AccountSkeleton from "./account-skeleton";

interface AuthGuardProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

export default function AuthGuard({ children }: AuthGuardProps) {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !user) {
      const returnUrl = encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname);
      router.replace(`/my-account?return=${returnUrl}`);
    }
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  if (loading) return <AccountSkeleton />;
  if (!user) return null; // Will redirect — show nothing while navigating

  return <>{children}</>;
}

import type { ReactElement } from "react";

export const CHECKOUT_STEPS: string[] = ["Shopping Cart", "Checkout", "Order Complete"];

interface CheckoutStepsProps {
  current: number;
}

export default function CheckoutSteps({ current }: CheckoutStepsProps): ReactElement {
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Checkout progress" className="w-full">
      <ol className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-x-2 gap-y-3 sm:gap-x-4 text-[12px] sm:text-[14px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide">
        {CHECKOUT_STEPS.map((step: string, index: number) => {
          const active: boolean = index === current;
          const done: boolean = index < current;
          const isLast: boolean = index === CHECKOUT_STEPS.length - 1;

          const circleClass: string = active
            ? "bg-[#e91e63] text-white"
            : done
              ? "bg-[#212529] text-white"
              : "bg-[#f1f5f9] text-[#6c757d]";

          const labelClass: string = active
            ? "text-[#e91e63]"
            : done
              ? "text-[#212529]"
              : "text-[#6c757d]";

          return (
            <li
              key={step}
              aria-current={active ? "step" : undefined}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-4"
            >
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span
                  className={`w-7 h-7 shrink-0 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[12px] font-bold ${circleClass}`}
                >
                  {index + 1}
                </span>
                <span className={labelClass}>{step}</span>
              </span>
              {!isLast && (
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="block w-4 sm:w-8 h-px bg-[#dee2e6]" />
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );
}

// components/field-error.tsx

interface FieldErrorProps {
  message?: string;
  id?: string;
}

export default function FieldError({ message, id }: FieldErrorProps) {
  if (!message) return null;
  return (
    <p id={id} className="mt-1 text-sm text-red-600" role="alert">
      {message}
    </p>
  );
}

================
File: components/product-search.tsx
================
// components/product-search.tsx
"use client";

import {
  useEffect,
  useId,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type FormEvent,
  type KeyboardEvent,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

/* ============================================================
   TYPES
   ============================================================ */
interface SearchProduct {
  id: string;
  databaseId: number;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  price: string | null;
  image: { sourceUrl: string } | null;
}

interface SearchResponse {
  data?: { products?: { nodes: SearchProduct[] } };
  errors?: Array<{ message: string }>;
}

interface ProductSearchProps {
  /** Called after a navigation so the parent can close the search bar. */
  onNavigate?: () => void;
  autoFocus?: boolean;
}

/* ============================================================
   CONSTANTS
   ============================================================ */
const API_URL: string =
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WORDPRESS_API_URL || "http://localhost/shophope/graphql";

const MIN_CHARS: number = 2;
const DEBOUNCE_MS: number = 300;
const SUGGESTION_LIMIT: number = 6;

const SEARCH_QUERY: string = `
  query SearchProducts($term: String!) {
    products(first: ${SUGGESTION_LIMIT}, where: { search: $term }) {
      nodes {
        id
        databaseId
        name
        slug
        image {
          sourceUrl
        }
        ... on SimpleProduct {
          price
        }
      }
    }
  }
`;

/* ============================================================
   HELPERS
   ============================================================ */
/** WooCommerce prices arrive as HTML. Convert them to plain text. */
function toPlainText(html: string | null): string {
  return html ? html.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, "").trim() : "";
}

/* ============================================================
   ICONS
   ============================================================ */
function SearchGlyph(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7" />
      <path d="m20 20-3.5-3.5" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function CloseGlyph(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.25" strokeLinecap="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M18 6 6 18M6 6l12 12" />
    </svg>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   COMPONENT
   ============================================================ */
export default function ProductSearch({ onNavigate, autoFocus = false }: ProductSearchProps): ReactNode {
  const router = useRouter();
  const inputId: string = useId();
  const listId: string = `${inputId}-results`;
  const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [query, setQuery] = useState<string>("");
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [results, setResults] = useState<SearchProduct[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState<number>(-1);

  const term: string = query.trim();
  const showPanel: boolean = open && term.length >= MIN_CHARS;

  /* Debounced fetch. Stale requests are aborted when the user keeps typing. */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (term.length < MIN_CHARS) {
      // Clear results when the query is too short.
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setResults([]);
      setError(null);
      setLoading(false);
      setActiveIndex(-1);
      return;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const timer: number = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(API_URL, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ query: SEARCH_QUERY, variables: { term } }),
          signal: controller.signal,
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Search failed with status ${res.status}`);

        const json: SearchResponse = await res.json();
        if (json.errors && json.errors.length > 0) throw new Error(json.errors[0].message);

        setResults(json.data?.products?.nodes ?? []);
        setActiveIndex(-1);
      } catch {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setResults([]);
        setError("Search is unavailable right now. Press Enter to search the full shop.");
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }, DEBOUNCE_MS);

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [term]);

  /* Close the suggestions when the user clicks outside the search box */
  useEffect(() => {
    function handlePointerDown(event: MouseEvent): void {
      if (!wrapperRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
        setActiveIndex(-1);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handlePointerDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handlePointerDown);
  }, []);

  function finish(): void {
    setOpen(false);
    setActiveIndex(-1);
    setQuery("");
    onNavigate?.();
  }

  function goToResults(): void {
    if (term === "") return;
    finish();
    router.push(`/shop?search=${encodeURIComponent(term)}`);
  }

  function clearQuery(): void {
    setQuery("");
    setOpen(false);
    inputRef.current?.focus();
  }

  function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>): void {
    event.preventDefault();
    const highlighted: SearchProduct | undefined = results[activeIndex];
    if (highlighted) {
      finish();
      router.push(`/product/${highlighted.slug}`);
      return;
    }
    goToResults();
  }

  function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void {
    if (event.key === "ArrowDown") {
      event.preventDefault();
      setOpen(true);
      setActiveIndex((i: number) => (results.length === 0 ? -1 : (i + 1) % results.length));
    } else if (event.key === "ArrowUp") {
      event.preventDefault();
      setActiveIndex((i: number) =>
        results.length === 0 ? -1 : i <= 0 ? results.length - 1 : i - 1
      );
    } else if (event.key === "Escape") {
      setOpen(false);
      setActiveIndex(-1);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div ref={wrapperRef} className="relative w-full">
      <form role="search" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-stretch gap-2">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <label htmlFor={inputId} className="sr-only">
            Search products
          </label>

          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#6c757d]">
            <SearchGlyph />
          </span>

          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            id={inputId}
            type="search"
            role="combobox"
            aria-autocomplete="list"
            aria-expanded={showPanel}
            aria-controls={listId}
            aria-activedescendant={activeIndex >= 0 ? `${listId}-${activeIndex}` : undefined}
            autoComplete="off"
            autoFocus={autoFocus}
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setQuery(e.target.value);
              setOpen(true);
            }}
            onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
            onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
            placeholder="Search for products, categories and more"
            className="h-12 w-full pl-11 pr-11 bg-white border border-[#dee2e6] rounded-md text-[15px] text-[#212529] placeholder:text-[#adb5bd] transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e91e63] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#e91e63]/15 [&::-webkit-search-cancel-button]:hidden"
          />

          {query !== "" && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={clearQuery}
              aria-label="Clear search"
              className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-8 h-8 inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[#6c757d] hover:text-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#fdf2f6] transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]"
            >
              <CloseGlyph />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="h-12 px-6 bg-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#c2185b] text-white text-[14px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide rounded-md transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
        >
          Search
        </button>
      </form>

      {showPanel && (
        <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-full mt-2 z-50 overflow-hidden bg-white border border-[#f1f5f9] rounded-md shadow-[0_10px_30px_rgba(33,37,41,0.12)]">
          {loading && (
            <div className="p-3 space-y-3" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Searching">
              {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i: number) => (
                <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-[#f8f9fa] animate-pulse" />
                  <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
                    <div className="h-3 w-3/4 bg-[#f8f9fa] animate-pulse" />
                    <div className="h-3 w-1/4 bg-[#f8f9fa] animate-pulse" />
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && error && (
            <p role="alert" className="px-4 py-4 text-sm text-[#6c757d]">
              {error}
            </p>
          )}

          {!loading && !error && results.length === 0 && (
            <p className="px-4 py-4 text-sm text-[#6c757d]">
              No products match <span className="font-semibold text-[#212529]">&quot;{term}&quot;</span>.
            </p>
          )}

          {!loading && results.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <div id={listId} role="listbox" aria-label="Search suggestions">
                {results.map((product: SearchProduct, index: number) => {
                  const price: string = toPlainText(product.price);
                  const active: boolean = index === activeIndex;
                  return (
                    <Link
                      key={product.id}
                      id={`${listId}-${index}`}
                      role="option"
                      aria-selected={active}
                      href={`/product/${product.slug}`}
                      onClick={finish}
                      onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIndex(index)}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 border-b border-[#f1f5f9] transition-colors ${
                        active ? "bg-[#fdf2f6]" : "bg-white"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="w-12 h-12 shrink-0 bg-[#f8f9fa] overflow-hidden">
                        {product.image?.sourceUrl && (
                          // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                          <img src={product.image.sourceUrl} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                        )}
                      </span>
                      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <span className="block truncate text-[15px] text-[#212529]">{product.name}</span>
                        {price && (
                          <span className="block mt-0.5 text-[14px] font-bold text-[#e91e63]">{price}</span>
                        )}
                      </span>
                    </Link>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={goToResults}
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 text-left text-[13px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#e91e63] hover:bg-[#fdf2f6] transition-colors"
              >
                View all results for &quot;{term}&quot;
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// components/site-header.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import ProductSearch from "./product-search";
import { WISHLIST_EVENT, readWishlist } from "@/lib/wishlist";
import { CART_EVENT, formatKD, readCart, type CartItem } from "@/lib/cart";
import { AUTH_EVENT, getStoredToken } from "@/lib/auth";

/* ============================================================
   CONSTANTS
   ============================================================ */
const LOGO_SRC: string = "/logo.png";

const NAV_LINKS: Array<{ label: string; href: string }> = [
  { label: "HOME", href: "/" },
  { label: "SHOP", href: "/shop" },
  { label: "TRACK ORDER", href: "/orders" }, // TODO: confirm; account orders live at /my-account/orders
];

const MOBILE_MENU_ID: string = "mobile-menu";

/* ============================================================
   ICONS
   ============================================================ */
function SearchIcon(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7" />
      <path d="m20 20-3.5-3.5" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function HeartIcon(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M20.8 4.6a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.8 0L12 5.7l-1-1.1a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.8 7.8l1 1.1L12 21l7.8-7.5 1-1.1a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0-7.8Z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function BasketIcon(): ReactNode {
  return (
    <svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M6 2 3 6v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h14a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V6l-3-4Z" />
      <path d="M3 6h18" />
      <path d="M16 10a4 4 0 0 1-8 0" />
    </svg>

}

/* ============================================================
   HEADER
   Reads cart, wishlist and login state from localStorage itself,
   so it needs no props and lives once in app/layout.tsx.
   ============================================================ */
export default function SiteHeader(): ReactNode {
  const pathname: string = usePathname();

  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [wishlistCount, setWishlistCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [cartItems, setCartItems] = useState<CartItem[]>([]);
  const [isLoggedIn, setIsLoggedIn] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [prevPathname, setPrevPathname] = useState<string>(pathname);

  const searchButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  /* Close the menu and the search bar whenever the page changes.
     State is adjusted during render (no effect needed), so there is no extra re-render pass. */
  if (pathname !== prevPathname) {
    setPrevPathname(pathname);
    setMenuOpen(false);
    setSearchOpen(false);
  }

  /* Wishlist count */
  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = (): void => setWishlistCount(readWishlist().length);
    sync();
    window.addEventListener(WISHLIST_EVENT, sync);
    window.addEventListener("storage", sync);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(WISHLIST_EVENT, sync);
      window.removeEventListener("storage", sync);
    };
  }, []);

  /* Cart count and total */
  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = (): void => setCartItems(readCart());
    sync();
    window.addEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync);
    window.addEventListener("storage", sync);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync);
      window.removeEventListener("storage", sync);
    };
  }, []);

  /* Login state (switches the label between LOGIN / REGISTER and MY ACCOUNT) */
  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = (): void => setIsLoggedIn(Boolean(getStoredToken()));
    sync();
    window.addEventListener(AUTH_EVENT, sync);
    window.addEventListener("storage", sync);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(AUTH_EVENT, sync);
      window.removeEventListener("storage", sync);
    };
  }, []);

  /* Escape closes the search bar and returns focus to its trigger */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!searchOpen) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setSearchOpen(false);
        searchButtonRef.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [searchOpen]);

  const cartCount: number = cartItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
  const cartTotal: number = cartItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.price * item.quantity, 0);
  const accountLabel: string = isLoggedIn ? "MY ACCOUNT" : "LOGIN / REGISTER";

  /* Highlights the link of the current page ("/" only matches the home page exactly) */
  const isActive = (href: string): boolean =>
    href === "/" ? pathname === "/" : pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`);

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 w-full bg-white">
      <div className="w-full h-[90px] px-4 lg:px-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <Link href="/" className="flex items-center shrink-0 h-full rounded focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63]" aria-label="Shop & Hope home">
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
          <img src={LOGO_SRC} alt="" className="h-[72px] w-auto max-w-[200px] object-contain" />
        </Link>

        <nav className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-8 h-full" aria-label="Main navigation">
          {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
            <Link
              key={link.href}
              href={link.href}
              aria-current={isActive(link.href) ? "page" : undefined}
              className={`text-[16px] font-medium uppercase transition-colors rounded focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] ${
                isActive(link.href) ? "text-[#e91e63]" : "text-[#212529] hover:text-[#e91e63]"
              }`}
            >
              {link.label}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-5">
          <Link
            href="/my-account"
            aria-current={isActive("/my-account") ? "page" : undefined}
            className={`hidden lg:inline text-[16px] font-medium uppercase transition-colors rounded focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] ${
              isActive("/my-account") ? "text-[#e91e63]" : "text-[#212529] hover:text-[#e91e63]"
            }`}
          >
            {accountLabel}
          </Link>

          <button
            ref={searchButtonRef}
            type="button"
            aria-label="Search"
            aria-expanded={searchOpen}
            aria-controls="header-search"
            onClick={() => setSearchOpen((prev: boolean) => !prev)}
            className="text-[#212529] hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] rounded"
          >
            <SearchIcon />
          </button>

          <Link
            href="/wishlist"
            aria-label={`Wishlist with ${wishlistCount} items`}
            className="relative text-[#212529] hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] rounded"
          >
            <span className="relative inline-flex">
              <HeartIcon />
              <span aria-hidden="true" className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 min-w-[16px] h-4 px-1 rounded-full bg-[#e91e63] text-white text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
                {wishlistCount}
              </span>
            </span>
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/cart"
            aria-label={`Cart with ${cartCount} items`}
            className="relative flex items-center gap-2 text-[#212529] hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] rounded"
          >
            <span className="relative">
              <BasketIcon />
              <span aria-hidden="true" className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 min-w-[16px] h-4 px-1 rounded-full bg-[#e91e63] text-white text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
                {cartCount}
              </span>
            </span>
            <span className="hidden sm:inline text-[16px] font-medium">{formatKD(cartTotal)}</span>
          </Link>

          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label={menuOpen ? "Close menu" : "Open menu"}
            aria-expanded={menuOpen}
            aria-controls={MOBILE_MENU_ID}
            onClick={() => setMenuOpen((prev: boolean) => !prev)}
            className="md:hidden text-[#212529] hover:text-[#e91e63] transition-colors focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] rounded"
          >
            <svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" aria-hidden="true">
              <path d="M3 6h18M3 12h18M3 18h18" />
            </svg>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Search bar (opens from the search icon) */}
      {searchOpen && (
        <div id="header-search" className="w-full border-t border-[#f1f5f9] bg-white px-4 lg:px-5 py-4">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1260px]">
            <ProductSearch autoFocus onNavigate={() => setSearchOpen(false)} />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Mobile menu */}
      {menuOpen && (
        <div id={MOBILE_MENU_ID} className="md:hidden border-t border-[#f1f5f9] bg-white">
          <div className="px-4 py-2 flex flex-col">
            {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
              <Link
                key={link.href}
                href={link.href}
                aria-current={isActive(link.href) ? "page" : undefined}
                className={`py-3 text-[14px] font-medium uppercase border-b border-[#f1f5f9] rounded focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] ${
                  isActive(link.href) ? "text-[#e91e63]" : "text-[#212529]"
                }`}
              >
                {link.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
            <Link
              href="/my-account"
              aria-current={isActive("/my-account") ? "page" : undefined}
              className={`py-3 text-[14px] font-medium uppercase rounded focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#e91e63] ${
                isActive("/my-account") ? "text-[#e91e63]" : "text-[#212529]"
              }`}
            >
              {accountLabel}
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

// components/submit-button.tsx
"use client";

import { type ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";

interface SubmitButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  loading: boolean;
  label: string;
  loadingLabel?: string;
}

export default function SubmitButton({
  loading,
  label,
  loadingLabel = "Saving…",
  disabled,
  ...rest
}: SubmitButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      type="submit"
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-[#e91e63] px-6 py-2.5 text-[14px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-white transition-colors hover:bg-[#c2185b] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#e91e63] focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
      {...rest}
    >
      {loading && (
        <svg
          className="-ml-1 mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin"
          fill="none"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <circle
            className="opacity-25"
            cx="12"
            cy="12"
            r="10"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="4"
          />
          <path
            className="opacity-75"
            fill="currentColor"
            d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4z"
          />
        </svg>
      )}
      {loading ? loadingLabel : label}
    </button>
  );
}

================
File: hooks/use-auth.ts
================
// hooks/use-auth.ts
"use client";
// ------------------------------------------------------------------
// React hook that tracks the current user and loading state.
// Re-runs on the custom AUTH_EVENT so the header updates instantly.
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { getViewer, getTokens, AUTH_EVENT, User } from "@/lib/auth";

interface AuthState {
  user: User | null;
  loading: boolean;
  refresh: () => Promise<void>;
}

export function useAuth(): AuthState {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const fetch = useCallback(async () => {
    const tokens = getTokens();
    if (!tokens) {
      setUser(null);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    try {
      const viewer = await getViewer();
      setUser(viewer);
    } catch {
      setUser(null);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Hydrate auth state from localStorage after mount.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    fetch();

    const handler = () => fetch();
    window.addEventListener(AUTH_EVENT, handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener(AUTH_EVENT, handler);
  }, [fetch]);

  return { user, loading, refresh: fetch };
}

================
File: lib/auth.ts

// lib/auth.ts
// ------------------------------------------------------------------
// Authentication, session management, and account queries for the
// My Account pages. Every exported action throws a GraphQLError whose
// message is short and safe to show to the user. Tokens and personal
// data are never logged.
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

import { graphqlRequest, ExpiredTokenError, GraphQLError } from "./graphql";

/* ── Constants ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */

const AUTH_TOKEN_KEY = "wp_auth_token";
const REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY = "wp_refresh_token";
export const AUTH_EVENT = "auth-state-change";

const MSG_NETWORK =
  "We could not reach the store. Check your connection and try again.";
const MSG_NOT_SIGNED_IN = "Please log in to continue.";
const MSG_SESSION_EXPIRED = "Your session has expired. Please log in again.";
const MSG_LINK_INVALID =
  "This reset link is invalid or has expired. Please request a new one.";
const MSG_RESET_FAILED = "Could not reset your password. Please try again.";

/* ── Tokens ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

export interface AuthTokens {
  authToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
}

export function getTokens(): AuthTokens | null {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  const authToken = localStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
  const refreshToken = localStorage.getItem(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY);
  return authToken && refreshToken ? { authToken, refreshToken } : null;
}

/** Returns the stored auth token, or null. Safe to call during SSR. */
export function getStoredToken(): string | null {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  return localStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
}

function setTokens(tokens: AuthTokens): void {
  localStorage.setItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, tokens.authToken);
  localStorage.setItem(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY, tokens.refreshToken);
}

function clearTokens(): void {
  localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
  localStorage.removeItem(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY);
}

function dispatchAuthEvent(): void {
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(AUTH_EVENT));
}

/* ── Types ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

export interface User {
  id: string;
  databaseId: number;
  username: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  firstName: string | null;
  lastName: string | null;
}

export interface Address {
  firstName?: string | null;
  lastName?: string | null;
  company?: string | null;
  address1?: string | null;
  address2?: string | null;
  city?: string | null;
  state?: string | null;
  postcode?: string | null;
  country?: string | null;
  phone?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
}

export interface Customer {
  id: string;
  firstName?: string | null;
  lastName?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
  billing?: Address | null;
  shipping?: Address | null;
}

export interface UpdateCustomerInput {
  firstName?: string;
  lastName?: string;
  email?: string;
  billing?: Address;
  shipping?: Address;
}

export interface UpdateUserInput {
  id: string;
  firstName?: string;
  lastName?: string;
  email?: string;
}

export interface Order {
  id: string;
  databaseId: number;
  orderNumber: string;
  date: string;
  status: string;
  /** Raw amount as a string, e.g. "12.500". Convert with Number() before formatKD. */
  total: string;
  currency?: string;
}

export interface OrderDetail extends Order {
  paymentMethodTitle?: string | null;
  lineItems?: {
    nodes: {
      product?: { name: string; id: string } | null;
      quantity: number;
      total: string;
    }[];
  };
  shipping?: {
    address1?: string | null;
    address2?: string | null;
    city?: string | null;
    state?: string | null;
    postcode?: string | null;
    country?: string | null;
  } | null;
  billing?: {
    address1?: string | null;
    address2?: string | null;
    city?: string | null;
    state?: string | null;
    postcode?: string | null;
    country?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    phone?: string | null;
  } | null;
}

/* ── Error mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────── */

interface MessageRule {
  pattern: RegExp;
  message: string;
}

/** Turns known WordPress messages into friendly text. */
const MESSAGE_RULES: ReadonlyArray<MessageRule> = [
  {
    pattern:
      /email.*(already|exists|registered|in use)|(already|exists|registered|in use).*email/i,
    message: "That email address is already registered.",
  },
  {
    pattern:
      /username.*(already|exists|in use|taken)|(already|exists|in use).*username/i,
    message: "That username is already taken.",
  },
  {
    pattern:
      /incorrect|invalid username|invalid email|unknown email|does not exist/i,
    message: "Incorrect username or password.",
  },
];

const MAX_RAW_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 160;

/**
 * Maps any thrown value to a short, user-facing message.
 * Known WordPress messages are rewritten; other GraphQL messages are shown
 * if they are short, and the fallback is used otherwise.
 */
export function toFriendlyMessage(err: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  if (err instanceof ExpiredTokenError) return MSG_SESSION_EXPIRED;
  if (!(err instanceof GraphQLError)) return fallback;

  for (const rule of MESSAGE_RULES) {
    if (rule.pattern.test(err.message)) return rule.message;
  }
  const raw = err.message.trim();
  return raw && raw.length <= MAX_RAW_MESSAGE_LENGTH ? raw : fallback;
}

/** Sends a request and converts network and parsing failures to GraphQLError. */
async function request<T>(
  query: string,
  variables?: Record<string, unknown>,
  token?: string,
): Promise<T> {
  try {
    return await graphqlRequest<T>(query, variables, token);
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    if (err instanceof GraphQLError) throw err;
    // fetch failed (offline, CORS, DNS), or the response was not JSON.
    throw new GraphQLError(MSG_NETWORK);
  }
}

/** Runs an action and rethrows any error with a friendly message. */
async function friendly<T>(
  fallback: string,
  action: () => Promise<T>,
): Promise<T> {
  try {
    return await action();
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    throw new GraphQLError(toFriendlyMessage(err, fallback));
  }
}

/* ── Session refresh ─────────────────────────────────────────── */

const REFRESH_MUTATION = /* GraphQL */ `
  mutation RefreshJwtAuthToken($input: RefreshJwtAuthTokenInput!) {
    refreshJwtAuthToken(input: $input) {
      authToken
    }
  }
`;

/**
 * Exchanges the stored refresh token for a new auth token.
 * Returns true when the new token is saved, false otherwise.
 */
export async function refreshSession(): Promise<boolean> {
  const tokens = getTokens();
  if (!tokens) return false;

  try {
    const data = await request<{
      refreshJwtAuthToken: { authToken: string } | null;
    }>(REFRESH_MUTATION, { input: { jwtRefreshToken: tokens.refreshToken } });

    const newAuthToken = data.refreshJwtAuthToken?.authToken;
    if (!newAuthToken) return false;

    setTokens({ authToken: newAuthToken, refreshToken: tokens.refreshToken });
    dispatchAuthEvent();
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Runs a query or mutation as the signed-in user. If the auth token has
 * expired, refreshes it once and retries once. Logs out if that fails.
 */
async function authedRequest<T>(
  query: string,
  variables?: Record<string, unknown>,
): Promise<T> {
  const tokens = getTokens();
  if (!tokens) throw new GraphQLError(MSG_NOT_SIGNED_IN);

  try {
    return await request<T>(query, variables, tokens.authToken);
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    if (!(err instanceof ExpiredTokenError)) throw err;
  }

  const refreshed = await refreshSession();
  const fresh = refreshed ? getTokens() : null;
  if (!fresh) {
    await logout();
    throw new GraphQLError(MSG_SESSION_EXPIRED);
  }

  try {
    return await request<T>(query, variables, fresh.authToken);
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    if (err instanceof ExpiredTokenError) {
      await logout();
      throw new GraphQLError(MSG_SESSION_EXPIRED);
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

/* ── Login, register, logout ─────────────────────────────────── */

const LOGIN_MUTATION = /* GraphQL */ `
  mutation LoginUser($input: LoginInput!) {
    login(input: $input) {
      authToken
      refreshToken
      user {
        id
        databaseId
        username
        name
        email
        firstName
        lastName
      }
    }
  }
`;

export function login(username: string, password: string): Promise<User> {
  return friendly("Login failed. Please try again.", async () => {
    const data = await request<{
      login: { authToken: string; refreshToken: string; user: User };
    }>(LOGIN_MUTATION, { input: { username, password } });

    setTokens({
      authToken: data.login.authToken,
      refreshToken: data.login.refreshToken,
    });
    dispatchAuthEvent();
    return data.login.user;
  });
}

const REGISTER_MUTATION = /* GraphQL */ `
  mutation RegisterUser($input: RegisterUserInput!) {
    registerUser(input: $input) {
      user {
        id
        databaseId
        username
      }
    }
  }
`;

/**
 * Creates the account, then signs the user in with the new credentials.
 * The core registerUser mutation does not return tokens, so login() is
 * called afterwards.
 */
export function register(
  username: string,
  email: string,
  password: string,
): Promise<User> {
  return friendly("Registration failed. Please try again.", async () => {
    await request(REGISTER_MUTATION, { input: { username, email, password } });
    return login(username, password);
  });
}

export async function logout(): Promise<void> {
  clearTokens();
  dispatchAuthEvent();
}

/* ── Viewer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

const VIEWER_QUERY = /* GraphQL */ `
  query Viewer {
    viewer {
      id
      databaseId
      username
      name
      email
      firstName
      lastName
    }
  }
`;

/** Returns the signed-in user, or throws if the session is invalid. */
export function getViewer(): Promise<User> {
  return friendly("Could not load your account.", async () => {
    const data = await authedRequest<{ viewer: User | null }>(VIEWER_QUERY);
    if (!data.viewer) throw new GraphQLError(MSG_SESSION_EXPIRED);
    return data.viewer;
  });
}

/* ── Password reset (core WPGraphQL mutations) ───────────────── */

const SEND_RESET_MUTATION = /* GraphQL */ `
  mutation SendPasswordResetEmail($input: SendPasswordResetEmailInput!) {
    sendPasswordResetEmail(input: $input) {
      user {
        id
      }
    }
  }
`;

/**
 * Requests a reset email. Returns false on any failure, so callers can show
 * the same confirmation whether or not the account exists.
 */
export async function sendPasswordResetEmail(username: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await request(SEND_RESET_MUTATION, { input: { username } });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

const RESET_PASSWORD_MUTATION = /* GraphQL */ `
  mutation ResetUserPassword($input: ResetUserPasswordInput!) {
    resetUserPassword(input: $input) {
      user {
        id
      }
    }
  }
`;

export async function resetUserPassword(
  key: string,
  login: string,
  password: string,
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await request(RESET_PASSWORD_MUTATION, { input: { key, login, password } });
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    // An expired key surfaces as an expiry error, so show the link message.
    if (err instanceof ExpiredTokenError) {
      throw new GraphQLError(MSG_LINK_INVALID);
    }
    throw new GraphQLError(toFriendlyMessage(err, MSG_RESET_FAILED));
  }
}

const ORDERS_QUERY = /* GraphQL */ `
  query Orders {
    orders {
      nodes {
        id
        databaseId
        orderNumber
        date
        status
        total(format: RAW)
      }
    }
  }
`;

const ORDER_QUERY = /* GraphQL */ `
  query Order($id: ID!) {
    order(id: $id, idType: DATABASE_ID) {
      id
      databaseId
      orderNumber
      date
      status
      total(format: RAW)
      paymentMethodTitle
      lineItems {
        nodes {
          product {
            id
            name
          }
          quantity
          total(format: RAW)
        }
      }
      shipping {
        address1
        address2
        city
        state
        postcode
        country
      }
      billing {
        address1
        address2
        city
        state
        postcode
        country
        email
        phone
      }
    }
  }
`;

export function getOrders(): Promise<Order[]> {
  return friendly("Could not load your orders.", async () => {
    const data = await authedRequest<{ orders: { nodes: Order[] } | null }>(
      ORDERS_QUERY,
    );
    return data.orders?.nodes ?? [];
  });
}

export function getOrder(id: string): Promise<OrderDetail> {
  return friendly("Could not load this order.", async () => {
    const data = await authedRequest<{ order: OrderDetail | null }>(
      ORDER_QUERY,
      { id },
    );
    // Returns null for orders that do not belong to the signed-in user.
    if (!data.order) throw new GraphQLError("Order not found.");
    return data.order;
  });
}

File: lib/cart.ts
