// lib/cart.ts
import { DELIVERY_FEE_KD } from "@/lib/orders";

/* ============================================================
   CONSTANTS
   ============================================================ */
export const CART_STORAGE_KEY: string = "shophope-cart";

/** Dispatched on the window whenever the cart changes in this tab. */
export const CART_EVENT: string = "shophope-cart-change";

export const CURRENCY: string = "KD";

const MIN_QUANTITY: number = 1;
const MAX_QUANTITY: number = 50;

/* ============================================================
   TYPES
   ============================================================ */
export interface CartItem {
  /** WPGraphQL global ID or database ID. Sent to the server as-is. */
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  image: string | null;
  /** Unit price in KD, for display only. The server re-reads the real price. */
  price: number;
  quantity: number;
}

export type CartItemInput = Omit<CartItem, "quantity">;

/* ============================================================
   HELPERS
   ============================================================ */
function isBrowser(): boolean {
  return typeof window !== "undefined";
}

function clampQuantity(value: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return MIN_QUANTITY;
  return Math.min(MAX_QUANTITY, Math.max(MIN_QUANTITY, Math.floor(value)));
}

/** Guards against corrupted or old data in localStorage. */
function isCartItem(value: unknown): value is CartItem {
  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return false;
  const v = value as Partial<CartItem>;
  return (
    typeof v.id === "string" &&
    v.id.length > 0 &&
    typeof v.slug === "string" &&
    typeof v.name === "string" &&
    (typeof v.image === "string" || v.image === null) &&
    typeof v.price === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(v.price) &&
    typeof v.quantity === "number" &&
    Number.isInteger(v.quantity) &&
    v.quantity >= MIN_QUANTITY &&
    v.quantity <= MAX_QUANTITY
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   READ / WRITE
   ============================================================ */

/** Returns the cart from localStorage. Returns [] on the server or when data is invalid. */
export function readCart(): CartItem[] {
  if (!isBrowser()) return [];
  try {
    const raw: string | null = window.localStorage.getItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
    return parsed.filter(isCartItem);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

/** Saves the cart and notifies every component in this tab. */
export function writeCart(items: CartItem[]): void {
  if (!isBrowser()) return;
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(items));
  } catch {
    /* Storage full or disabled (private mode). Cart stays in memory for this page only. */
  }
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CART_EVENT));
}

/* ============================================================
   ACTIONS
   ============================================================ */

/** Adds a product. If it is already in the cart, the quantity is increased. */
export function addToCart(input: CartItemInput, quantity: number = 1): void {
  const items: CartItem[] = readCart();
  const addQty: number = clampQuantity(quantity);
  const existing: CartItem | undefined = items.find((i) => i.id === input.id);

  if (existing) {
    existing.quantity = clampQuantity(existing.quantity + addQty);
    existing.price = input.price;
    existing.name = input.name;
    existing.image = input.image;
    existing.slug = input.slug;
  } else {
    items.push({ ...input, quantity: clampQuantity(addQty) });
  }

  writeCart(items);
}

/** Sets an exact quantity (1 to 50). Values outside the range are clamped. */
export function updateCartQuantity(id: string, quantity: number): void {
  const items: CartItem[] = readCart().map((item) =>
    item.id === id ? { ...item, quantity: clampQuantity(quantity) } : item
  );
  writeCart(items);
}

export function removeFromCart(id: string): void {
  writeCart(readCart().filter((item) => item.id !== id));
}

export function clearCart(): void {
  writeCart([]);
}

/* ============================================================
   TOTALS
   ============================================================ */
export function getCartCount(items: CartItem[]): number {
  return items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
}

export function getCartSubtotal(items: CartItem[]): number {
  const total: number = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.price * item.quantity, 0);
  return Math.round(total * 1000) / 1000;
}

/** Flat delivery fee (KD 1.000). Same value is used by the checkout and the server. */
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars
export function getStandardShipping(_subtotal: number): number {
  return DELIVERY_FEE_KD;
}

/* ============================================================
   FORMATTING
   ============================================================ */

/** Kuwait prices use 3 decimals (fils). Example: KD 1.000 */
export function formatKD(amount: number): string {
  return `${CURRENCY} ${amount.toFixed(3)}`;
}

// lib/checkout-validation.ts
import { KUWAIT_GOVERNORATES, type CheckoutCustomer } from "@/lib/orders";

export type CustomerFieldErrors = Partial<Record<keyof CheckoutCustomer, string>>;

export const EMAIL_RE: RegExp = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
const KUWAIT_MOBILE_RE: RegExp = /^[569]\d{7}$/;

/** Strips spaces, dashes and brackets, then the +965 / 00965 / 965 prefix. */
export function normalizeKuwaitPhone(value: string): string {
  const digits: string = value.replace(/[\s\-()]/g, "");
  if (digits.startsWith("+965")) return digits.slice(4);
  if (digits.startsWith("00965")) return digits.slice(5);
  if (digits.startsWith("965") && digits.length === 11) return digits.slice(3);
  return digits;
}

/**
 * Validates the customer block. Returns clean data when valid, or field errors when not.
 * Never throws, so it is safe to use on untrusted input.
 */
export function validateCheckoutCustomer(input: unknown): {
  data: CheckoutCustomer | null;
  errors: CustomerFieldErrors;
} {
  const src: Record<string, unknown> =
    typeof input === "object" && input !== null ? (input as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
  const errors: CustomerFieldErrors = {};
  const clean: Record<string, string> = {};

  const readText = (key: keyof CheckoutCustomer, max: number, required: boolean): void => {
    const raw: unknown = src[key];
    const value: string = typeof raw === "string" ? raw.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ") : "";
    if (required && value.length === 0) errors[key] = "This field is required.";
    else if (value.length > max) errors[key] = `Must be ${max} characters or fewer.`;
    clean[key] = value;
  };

  readText("firstName", 60, true);
  readText("lastName", 60, true);
  readText("governorate", 40, true);
  if (!errors.governorate && !KUWAIT_GOVERNORATES.includes(clean.governorate)) {
    errors.governorate = "Select a valid governorate.";
  }
  readText("area", 80, true);
  readText("block", 20, true);
  readText("street", 100, true);
  readText("building", 20, true);
  readText("floor", 20, false);
  readText("apartment", 20, false);
  readText("deliveryNote", 500, false);

  const email: string = typeof src.email === "string" ? src.email.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
  if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email) || email.length > 120) {
    errors.email = "Enter a valid email address.";
  }

  const digits: string = typeof src.phone === "string" ? normalizeKuwaitPhone(src.phone.trim()) : "";
  if (!KUWAIT_MOBILE_RE.test(digits)) {
    errors.phone = "Enter an 8-digit Kuwait mobile number.";
  }

  if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return { data: null, errors };

  return {
    data: {
      firstName: clean.firstName,
      lastName: clean.lastName,
      email,
      phone: `+965${digits}`,
      governorate: clean.governorate,
      area: clean.area,
      block: clean.block,
      street: clean.street,
      building: clean.building,
      floor: clean.floor || undefined,
      apartment: clean.apartment || undefined,
      deliveryNote: clean.deliveryNote || undefined,
    },
    errors: {},
  };
}

// lib/graphql.ts
// ------------------------------------------------------------------
// Thin fetch wrapper for WPGraphQL. Handles auth headers and strips
// HTML from WordPress error messages. Used by lib/auth.ts and any
// page that needs to call the API directly.
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

export class GraphQLError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "GraphQLError";
  }
}

export class ExpiredTokenError extends GraphQLError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ExpiredTokenError";
  }
}

/** Remove HTML tags from WordPress error messages. */
function stripHtml(html: string): string {
  if (typeof document !== "undefined") {
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.innerHTML = html;
    return div.textContent || div.innerText || "";
  }
  return html.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, "");
}

export interface GraphQLResponse<T> {
  data: T | null;
  errors?: { message: string }[];
}

export async function graphqlRequest<T>(
  query: string,
  variables?: Record<string, unknown>,
  token?: string,
): Promise<T> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    "Content-Type": "application/json",
  };
  if (token) {
    headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  const res = await fetch(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WORDPRESS_API_URL!, {
    method: "POST",
    headers,
    body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables }),
  });

  const json: GraphQLResponse<T> = await res.json();

  if (json.errors?.length) {
    const msg = stripHtml(json.errors[0].message);

    // Detect expired / invalid JWT so callers can refresh
    if (
      /expired|jwt.*invalid|invalid.*token|jwt.*expired/i.test(msg)
    ) {
      throw new ExpiredTokenError(msg);
    }
    throw new GraphQLError(msg);
  }

  if (!json.data) {
    throw new GraphQLError("No data returned from the server.");
  }

  return json.data;
}

// lib/money.ts
/** Converts a WooCommerce RAW amount string to a number for formatKD. */
export function toAmount(raw: string | null | undefined): number {
  const n = Number(raw);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}

// lib/validators.ts
// ------------------------------------------------------------------
// Shared validation helpers. WordPress does final validation on the
// server; these give immediate client-side feedback.
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

export interface ValidationResult {
  valid: boolean;
  errors: Record<string, string>;
}

const EMAIL_PATTERN = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

export function validateLoginForm(fields: {
  username: string;
  password: string;
}): ValidationResult {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!fields.username.trim()) errors.username = "Username is required.";
  if (!fields.password) errors.password = "Password is required.";
  return { valid: Object.keys(errors).length === 0, errors };
}

export function validateRegisterForm(fields: {
  username: string;
  email: string;
  password: string;
}): ValidationResult {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!fields.username.trim()) errors.username = "Username is required.";
  if (!fields.email.trim()) {
    errors.email = "Email is required.";
  } else if (!EMAIL_PATTERN.test(fields.email.trim())) {
    errors.email = "Enter a valid email address.";
  }
  if (!fields.password) {
    errors.password = "Password is required.";
  } else if (fields.password.length < 8) {
    errors.password = "Password must be at least 8 characters.";
  }
  return { valid: Object.keys(errors).length === 0, errors };
}

export function validateDetailsForm(fields: {
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  email: string;
}): ValidationResult {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!fields.email.trim()) {
    errors.email = "Email is required.";
  } else if (!EMAIL_PATTERN.test(fields.email.trim())) {
    errors.email = "Enter a valid email address.";
  }
  return { valid: Object.keys(errors).length === 0, errors };
}

export function validatePasswordChange(fields: {
  currentPassword: string;
  newPassword: string;
  confirmPassword: string;
}): ValidationResult {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!fields.currentPassword)
    errors.currentPassword = "Current password is required.";
  if (!fields.newPassword) {
    errors.newPassword = "New password is required.";
  } else if (fields.newPassword.length < 8) {
    errors.newPassword = "Password must be at least 8 characters.";
  }
  if (fields.newPassword !== fields.confirmPassword) {
    errors.confirmPassword = "Passwords do not match.";
  }
  return { valid: Object.keys(errors).length === 0, errors };
}

export function validateAddressForm(fields: {
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  address1: string;
  city: string;
  postcode: string;
  country: string;
}): ValidationResult {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!fields.firstName.trim()) errors.firstName = "First name is required.";
  if (!fields.lastName.trim()) errors.lastName = "Last name is required.";
  if (!fields.address1.trim()) errors.address1 = "Address is required.";
  if (!fields.city.trim()) errors.city = "City is required.";
  if (!fields.postcode.trim()) errors.postcode = "Postcode is required.";
  if (!fields.country.trim()) errors.country = "Country is required.";
  return { valid: Object.keys(errors).length === 0, errors };
}

export function validateForgotPasswordForm(fields: {
  username: string;
}): ValidationResult {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!fields.username.trim())
    errors.username = "Username or email is required.";
  return { valid: Object.keys(errors).length === 0, errors };
}

export function validateResetPasswordForm(fields: {
  password: string;
  confirmPassword: string;
}): ValidationResult {
  const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!fields.password) {
    errors.password = "Password is required.";
  } else if (fields.password.length < 8) {
    errors.password = "Password must be at least 8 characters.";
  }
  if (fields.password !== fields.confirmPassword) {
    errors.confirmPassword = "Passwords do not match.";
  }
  return { valid: Object.keys(errors).length === 0, errors };
}

File: lib/wishlist.ts

// lib/wishlist.ts
export interface WishlistItem {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  image: string | null;
  price: number;
}

export const WISHLIST_KEY: string = "shophope-wishlist";
export const WISHLIST_EVENT: string = "shophope-wishlist-updated";

export function readWishlist(): WishlistItem[] {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
  try {
    const raw: string | null = localStorage.getItem(WISHLIST_KEY);
    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as WishlistItem[]) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function writeWishlist(items: WishlistItem[]): void {
  localStorage.setItem(WISHLIST_KEY, JSON.stringify(items));
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(WISHLIST_EVENT));
}

export function toggleWishlist(item: WishlistItem): boolean {
  const items: WishlistItem[] = readWishlist();
  const exists: boolean = items.some((i) => i.id === item.id);
  const next: WishlistItem[] = exists ? items.filter((i) => i.id !== item.id) : [...items, item];
  writeWishlist(next);
  return !exists;
}

File: lib/woocommerce.ts

// lib/woocommerce.ts
// Server-side only. Never import this from a client component.
import OAuth from "oauth-1.0a";
import crypto from "crypto";

const WC_URL: string | undefined = process.env.WOOCOMMERCE_URL;
const WC_KEY: string | undefined = process.env.WC_CONSUMER_KEY;
const WC_SECRET: string | undefined = process.env.WC_CONSUMER_SECRET;

export class WooError extends Error {
  status: number;
  constructor(message: string, status: number) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "WooError";
    this.status = status;
  }
}

// OAuth 1.0a signs each request. This is what WooCommerce expects over plain HTTP.
const oauth: OAuth | null =
  WC_KEY && WC_SECRET
    ? new OAuth({
        consumer: { key: WC_KEY, secret: WC_SECRET },
        signature_method: "HMAC-SHA256",
        hash_function(baseString: string, key: string): string {
          return crypto.createHmac("sha256", key).update(baseString).digest("base64");
        },
      })
    : null;

export async function wcFetch<T>(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
  if (!WC_URL || !oauth) {
    throw new WooError("WooCommerce environment variables are missing", 500);
  }

  const base: string = WC_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
  const cleanPath: string = path.replace(/^\//, "");
  const url: string = `${base}/wp-json/wc/v3/${cleanPath}`;
  const method: string = (init.method ?? "GET").toUpperCase();

  // Sign the method and full URL (including any ?query). The JSON body is not part of the signature.
  const signed = oauth.toHeader(oauth.authorize({ url, method }));
  const authHeader: { Authorization: string } = { Authorization: signed.Authorization };

  const res: Response = await fetch(url, {
    ...init,
    method,
    cache: "no-store",
    signal: init.signal ?? AbortSignal.timeout(15_000),
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      ...authHeader,
      ...(init.headers ?? {}),
    },
  });

  const data: unknown = await res.json().catch(() => null);

  if (!res.ok) {
    const message: string =
      typeof data === "object" && data !== null && "message" in data
        ? String((data as { message: unknown }).message)
        : `WooCommerce request failed (${res.status})`;
    throw new WooError(message, res.status);
  }

  return data as T;
}
